# Platform, evaluation and documentation subsystems

Reference detail for 5 subsystems. Entry point and cross-cutting analysis: [`../CODEBASE_ANALYSIS.md`](../CODEBASE_ANALYSIS.md).

Anchors are `file:line` as of 2026-09-22 and will drift; re-grep rather than assume a contradiction.

**Contents:** [`be-scripts`](#1-be-scripts---backend-operational-cli-scripts) &nbsp;|&nbsp; [`evaluation`](#2-evaluation---objective-2-evaluation-harness-jmeter-and-evidence-exports) &nbsp;|&nbsp; [`be-tests`](#3-be-tests---backend-test-suite-what-is-actually-pinned) &nbsp;|&nbsp; [`docs-paper`](#4-docs-paper---documentation-paper-evidence-and-governance) &nbsp;|&nbsp; [`ci-repo`](#5-ci-repo---ci-quality-gates-and-repository-hygiene)

## 1. `be-scripts` - Backend operational CLI scripts

Twenty-three operator-run entrypoints that live outside the API and worker processes: release/evidence generators (release_fingerprint, export_openapi, export_objective2_summary, gemini_release_smoke, catalog_live_smoke, verify_key_pool), PI-08 governance tooling (corpus_manifest, build_redacted_derivatives), data-plane repair/backfill/reindex CLIs that write to the live Supabase project (reindex_citations, repair_chunk_sections, rescan_duplication, backfill_legacy_locations, backfill_screening_archive_size), a synthetic-corpus seeder for load testing, and an encrypted backup/restore stack (storage_backup.py plus seven PowerShell wrappers). Five repo-root PowerShell verifiers produce the dated PI-03/PI-04 evidence bundles under docs/evidence/. None of them is invoked by CI; every one is run by hand by an operator, and most of them exist to produce a hashed artifact a defense panel reads.

### Key files

- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/release_fingerprint.py` (158 lines) - Schema-4 PI-03 provenance manifest: runtime, models, prompt version, generation contract, gateway route, RAG contract, index fingerprint, and SHA-256 of 11 pinned input files. Also imported as a library by evaluation/run_comparison.py:69.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/reindex_citations.py` (341 lines) - The only script that rebuilds vectors. Dry-run default; --apply stages a new index_version, verifies it, then flips it with activate_paper_index. Resumable via a gitignored state file.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/corpus_manifest.py` (462 lines) - PI-08 corpus validate/lock/verify. Canonical-JSON SHA-256 receipt, exclusive-create writes, 12-paper census enforcement, four institutional approvals.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/seed_synthetic_corpus.py` (625 lines) - Fabricates up to 15 obviously-labelled synthetic theses into a disposable Supabase project through commit_paper_ingestion, for /chat load testing. Refuses any target that is not provably disposable.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/storage_backup.py` (249 lines) - AES-256-GCM + scrypt encrypted tar of the pdfs and avatars buckets; verify and localhost-only restore subcommands. Path-traversal hardened.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/build_redacted_derivatives.py` (181 lines) - Runs the real extract_document over staged CCSICT-*.pdf to emit reviewable .redacted.txt derivatives plus redaction_receipts.json (redacted_sha256 and embedded_payload_sha256). Fail-closed on missing OCR.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/export_objective2_summary.py` (156 lines) - Generates rag-thesis-frontend/src/data/objective2Summary.json from one pinned comparison run so the admin Overview card cannot carry hand-typed figures.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/rescan_duplication.py` (118 lines) - Re-screens ready papers against today's archive using stored embeddings; nests the result under duplication_scan.rescan and never overwrites the at-upload record.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/repair_chunk_sections.py` (135 lines) - Re-derives chunks.section from the archived PDF, writing only that column and only when every stored chunk's content reproduces byte for byte.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/backfill_legacy_locations.py` (205 lines) - Shingle+anchor alignment of legacy chunks to PDF pages/sections; writes only chunks.metadata, never the columns.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/backfill_screening_archive_size.py` (103 lines) - Derives archive_size_estimated from upload order for screenings taken before archive_size was recorded (2026-09-14).
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/export_openapi.py` (36 lines) - Deterministic sorted-keys OpenAPI snapshot + sha256, gated by tests/test_export_openapi.py against docs/evidence/contracts/iskai-openapi.current.json.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/gemini_release_smoke.py` (125 lines) - The only script COPYed into the container image (Dockerfile:61). Live chat/verdict/embedding smoke with synthetic input; retains no response content.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/verify_key_pool.py` (124 lines) - One minimal live call per configured Gemini key; masked fingerprints only; exit 0 only when every key answers.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/catalog_live_smoke.py` (74 lines) - In-process TestClient GET /catalog/departments against a disposable project; asserts contract_version 2026-07-25 and the exact program/specialization sets.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/backup_system.ps1` (94 lines) - Three npx supabase db dump calls (roles/schema/data) plus storage_backup.py backup, then a sha256-manifest.json over the folder.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/scheduled_backup.ps1` (71 lines) - Unattended wrapper: DPAPI passphrase -> BACKUP_PASSPHRASE, transcript log, manifest-or-fail gate, best-effort record_backup_run, KeepLast pruning.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/record_backup_run.ps1` (118 lines) - POSTs to the record_backup_run RPC with backup id, completion time, artifact count, bytes, manifest digest and an opaque host fingerprint. Feeds the backup_stale alert.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/register_backup_task.ps1` (102 lines) - Registers the Windows Scheduled Task; distinguishes Password (unattended) from InteractiveToken by decrypted password length, never S4U.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/check_backup_freshness.ps1` (36 lines) - Exit 1 when the newest stamp-named folder carrying a sha256-manifest.json is older than -MaxAgeHours (default 48).
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/restore_database_local.ps1` (27 lines) - psql restore of roles/schema/data inside postgres:17-alpine against a localhost-only URI, then papers/chunks/orphan counts.
- `rag-thesis-backend/scripts/restore_storage_local.ps1` (26 lines) - storage_backup.py verify then restore-local against a localhost-only Supabase URL.
- `scripts/verify-pi03-container-security.ps1` (116 lines) - Trivy 0.72.0 HIGH/CRITICAL scan of api/worker/frontend images; zero-tolerance; writes docs/evidence/security/pi-03-<stamp>/ with SHA256SUMS.txt.
- `scripts/verify-pi03-dependencies.ps1` (131 lines) - pip-audit inside the release image + npm audit --omit=dev with a three-attempt metadata-presence guard.
- `scripts/verify-pi03-multiarch.ps1` (189 lines) - buildx linux/amd64+arm64 to an OCI tarball, independently re-hashes every OCI blob it reads, asserts both platforms present.
- `scripts/verify-pi03-gemini.ps1` (93 lines) - Rebuilds api/worker via docker-compose.operations.yml, runs gemini_release_smoke.py in-container with APP_ENVIRONMENT=development, asserts the exact model set.
- `scripts/verify-pi04-live.ps1` (80 lines) - Guards the disposable-project env, swaps SUPABASE_URL/KEY for the run, executes catalog_live_smoke.py, writes docs/evidence/pi-04/live-<stamp>/.

### Control flow

release_fingerprint.py is the provenance spine and is used two ways. As a CLI it prints a sorted-key JSON manifest; as a library, evaluation/run_comparison.py:898 calls build_manifest() exactly once per run and feeds it both to guard_checkpoint_provenance (which compares the subset named at run_comparison.py:415-419 — generation_route, rag_contract, index_fingerprint, input_sha256 — and expands input_sha256 to the individual file that moved, run_comparison.py:427-444) and to reproducibility.release in the published comparison JSON. build_manifest (release_fingerprint.py:50-141) reads settings for models, generation_contract, generation_route and rag_contract; calls services.index_provenance.current_index_fingerprint() for index_fingerprint; and SHA-256s exactly eleven paths (release_fingerprint.py:51-72): backend requirements.txt, requirements.lock, config.py, routers/chat.py, services/prompts.py, services/retriever.py, services/question_types.py, frontend package-lock.json, both Dockerfiles, and docker-compose.operations.yml. git_commit() shells `git rev-parse HEAD` with cwd=ROOT (parents[2] = repo root) and returns None on OSError/CalledProcessError, so a manifest built in the container records git_commit: null. gateway_host() (release_fingerprint.py:37-47) deliberately urlsplits LLM_BASE_URL down to .hostname, discarding userinfo, port, path and query, because this artifact is committed; tests/test_release_fingerprint.py:85-96 asserts a credential-bearing base URL leaks nothing.

The reindex path is the only script that can move vectors and is built as stage-then-flip. main() (reindex_citations.py:315-337) short-circuits to a dry-run report unless --apply, and the Gemini embedder import is deliberately inside apply_paper (reindex_citations.py:180) so a dry run cannot even construct an embedding client; the Supabase client import (`from services.retriever import sb`, line 331) is likewise apply-only. run_apply (261-312) rejects --apply without a target, loads the resume state and refuses it if state['index_fingerprint'] != current_index_fingerprint(), then iterates fetch_papers. Per paper, apply_paper (162-258) first reads the active row from paper_index_versions; a paper with active_index_version but no provenance row aborts ("apply the Item 34 migration first"); an incompatible embedding model needs --allow-model-change; an already-compatible index returns None and is recorded as skipped-but-completed. Otherwise it downloads from the pdfs bucket, re-extracts and re-chunks, embeds, calls verify_records (sequential chunk_index, non-empty content, token_count <= chunk_size_tokens, tokenizer/chunking_version provenance, complete and ordered page ranges, embedding count and 768 dimensions), inserts a fresh uuid4 index_version into paper_index_versions, inserts chunks in batches of 100, reads the staged rows back and re-checks count, distinct chunk_index, per-row tokenizer metadata, and that the stored provenance row equals staged_fingerprint exactly — and only then calls activate_paper_index. Any exception re-reads papers.active_index_version and, only if activation did not land, deletes the staged chunks and version rows before re-raising. run_apply catches per-paper failures into state['failed'] and keeps going; main returns 1 if that dict is non-empty.

The three 2026-09-14 repair scripts all share the same shape: dry-run default, --apply to write, repeatable --paper-id, stdout reconfigured to UTF-8 because thesis titles carry typographic characters and a Windows console is cp1252. repair_chunk_sections (62-104) selects chunks by paper_id AND index_version = active_index_version (the comment at 70-75 records that selecting on paper_id alone returned 7 rows for a paper holding both a legacy-char-v0 and a token-v1 index), re-derives records, and refuses to write unless the chunk count matches and every stored content reproduces byte for byte; it then updates only chunks.section, one row at a time. rescan_duplication (58-87) delegates to services.novelty.rescan_indexed_paper, which scores stored vectors directly rather than going through match_chunks (novelty.py:383-392 explains why: with the paper already indexed, self-matches at 1.0 and 0.9x neighbours fill any match_count), and persists through services.novelty.store_rescan, which nests the new result under duplication_scan.rescan and preserves the at-upload keys. backfill_screening_archive_size (80-99) pulls every ready paper ordered by created_at, counts same-department predecessors by created_at string comparison, and writes archive_size_estimated only where archive_size is absent.

The PI-08 governance pair is hash-driven. build_redacted_derivatives refuses to run at all when services.document_processor.OCR_AVAILABLE is false (110-115) and refuses per-manuscript when document.unresolved_scanned_pages is non-empty (77-84), because the receipt would certify text the container would index differently; it emits <stem>.redacted.txt plus redaction_receipts.json carrying both redacted_sha256 (over the derivative text) and embedded_payload_sha256 (over the newline-joined post-noise-filter chunk contents — the exact bytes that reach the provider). corpus_manifest then validates a hand-maintained working manifest: schema_version 1, department CCSICT, expected_paper_count 12, processing_profile forced to the unpaid Gemini tier and redacted-only content, per-paper CCSICT-NNN ids, UUID paper_ids, three SHA-256 digests each, unpaid_gemini_eligible === true, and a released_composition block whose represented counts must sum to expected_paper_count and, once lock-ready, match the actual papers[] distribution exactly. lock requires status=approved, revalidates with lock_ready=True, sorts papers by record_id, stamps locked_at, and writes manifest and receipt with open(mode='x') so neither can overwrite an existing artifact; if the receipt write fails the manifest is unlinked. verify recomputes the canonical digest (compact separators, sorted keys, ensure_ascii=False) and compares it to the receipt.

The backup stack is a PowerShell shell around one Python tool. scheduled_backup.ps1 decrypts a DPAPI file into $env:BACKUP_PASSPHRASE, invokes backup_system.ps1, treats a missing sha256-manifest.json as failure, best-effort calls record_backup_run.ps1 (a failure there only warns), then prunes to -KeepLast. backup_system.ps1 parses SUPABASE_URL/SUPABASE_KEY out of the backend .env with its own regex reader, validates the URL against ^https://[^/]+\.supabase\.co/?$ and rejects a `your-` placeholder key, runs three `npx.cmd supabase db dump` calls from the repository root (roles-only, schema, data with --use-copy and two storage vector tables excluded), then exports SUPABASE_BACKUP_KEY and runs storage_backup.py backup. storage_backup.backup walks both buckets with a recursive lister, downloads each object, writes objects/<bucket>/<path> through _object_file (which rejects unknown buckets, absolute paths and any '.'/'..' segment and re-resolves against the bucket root), builds manifest.json, tars it, and encrypts with AES-256-GCM under a scrypt(n=2**15,r=8,p=1) key, prefixing MAGIC+salt+nonce and appending the GCM tag. verify and restore-local both go through unpack_verified -> _decrypt -> tarfile.extractall(filter='data') -> _verify_tree (rejects a bad format, duplicate object keys, and any object whose SHA-256 does not match). restore additionally refuses any host outside {localhost, 127.0.0.1, host.docker.internal} before create_client is even reached.

The five repo-root verifiers all follow one template: compute a timestamped evidence directory under docs/evidence/, run the real tool, parse its machine-readable output, throw on any non-PASS, write SUMMARY.md, then SHA-256 every file in the directory into SHA256SUMS.txt (excluding itself). verify-pi03-gemini.ps1 rebuilds api and worker through docker-compose.operations.yml and then overrides APP_ENVIRONMENT=development for the run, because the compose file pins production and config.validate_production_services would refuse to construct Settings without Redis, MFA, ClamAV and a guest token budget — none of which the smoke exercises; it then asserts the four model/dimension constants against the report rather than against constants in the script. verify-pi03-multiarch.ps1 is the most self-contained: it buildx-builds to an OCI tarball with provenance and SBOM, extracts it, and walks index.json with Read-OciBlob, which re-hashes each blob and throws on a digest mismatch before parsing it, so the platform assertion rests on verified bytes; the worker row is synthesized from the api result with an explicit shared_image_contract note rather than built twice.

### Public surface

- python -m scripts.release_fingerprint [--output PATH] -> schema_version 4 JSON on stdout or file; exit 0 always (release_fingerprint.py:144-154)
- scripts.release_fingerprint.build_manifest() -> dict | sha256_file(Path) -> str | gateway_host() -> str|None | ROOT: Path — imported by evaluation/run_comparison.py:69,898 and tests/test_prompt_contracts.py:83
- python -m scripts.export_openapi <output.json> -> writes LF JSON, prints {openapi, output, sha256}; scripts.export_openapi.render_openapi() -> str (export_openapi.py:13-32)
- python -m scripts.export_objective2_summary -> writes rag-thesis-frontend/src/data/objective2Summary.json; exit 1 if the pinned run or its checkpoint provenance is missing. build(source=SOURCE) -> dict; constants SOURCE/RUN_ID='5e8fb7f21db6'/RUN_DATE='2026-09-13'/QUOTED_STRATUM='present'/OUT (export_objective2_summary.py:37-46,126-152)
- python -m scripts.corpus_manifest validate <manifest> [--lock-ready] | lock <manifest> --manifest-output P --receipt-output P [--locked-at ISO] | verify <manifest> <receipt>; exit 1 on ManifestError (corpus_manifest.py:414-458)
- scripts.corpus_manifest.validate_manifest(manifest, *, lock_ready=False) -> list[str]; lock_manifest(src, mp, rp, *, locked_at=None) -> (locked, receipt); verify_lock(manifest, receipt) -> None; manifest_sha256(dict) -> str; canonical_bytes(dict) -> bytes; ManifestError; EXPECTED_PAPER_COUNT=12; APPROVAL_ROLES; PROGRAM_SPECIALIZATIONS
- python -m scripts.reindex_citations [--paper-id ID | --all] [--apply] [--allow-model-change] [--resume] [--prune-old] [--older-than-days N=7] [--fixture-dir DIR]; exit 1 when any paper failed (reindex_citations.py:39-53,315-337)
- scripts.reindex_citations.apply_paper(client, paper, *, allow_model_change=False) -> dict|None; run_apply(args, client, state_path=STATE_FILE) -> dict; verify_records(records, embeddings=None); build_records(bytes, filename) -> list[dict]; dry_run_fixtures(Path) -> list[dict]; load_state/save_state; STATE_FILE
- python -m scripts.repair_chunk_sections [--apply] [--paper-id ID ...]; repair_paper(paper, apply_changes) -> dict (repair_chunk_sections.py:44-131)
- python -m scripts.rescan_duplication [--apply] [--paper-id ID ...]; rescan_paper(paper, apply_changes) -> dict (rescan_duplication.py:41-114)
- python -m scripts.backfill_screening_archive_size [--apply]; estimate_archive_sizes(papers) -> dict[str,int]; plan_backfill(papers) -> list[tuple[dict,int]] (backfill_screening_archive_size.py:39-99)
- python -m scripts.backfill_legacy_locations --paper-id ID --file PDF [--apply] [--minimum-coverage 0.70]; align_chunks(document, chunks, max_page_span=5) -> list[dict]; validate_alignments(alignments, minimum_coverage=0.70) (backfill_legacy_locations.py:65-201)
- python -m scripts.seed_synthetic_corpus --project-ref REF [--count 12] [--department D] [--seed 20260804] [--dry-run]; exit 2 on a refused target or bad --count (seed_synthetic_corpus.py:421-621). Module surface: TOPICS, TITLE_MARKER, build_thesis_text, paginate, read_env_file, resolve_target
- python -m scripts.build_redacted_derivatives [--corpus-dir DIR] [--output-dir DIR] [--force]; build_derivative(pdf) -> (text, receipt); build_all(corpus_dir, output_dir, *, force) -> dict; DerivativeError (build_redacted_derivatives.py:74-177)
- python scripts/storage_backup.py backup --url URL [--key K] --output FILE | verify --input FILE | restore-local --url URL [--key K] --input FILE (storage_backup.py:228-249). Internals used by tests: _encrypt/_decrypt/_object_file/_verify_tree/unpack_verified/backup/verify/restore; BUCKETS=('pdfs','avatars')
- python -m scripts.verify_key_pool [--output JSON]; exit 1 if GEMINI_API_KEY unset or any key fails (verify_key_pool.py:81-120)
- python scripts/gemini_release_smoke.py [--output JSON]; exit 1 on any failed check (gemini_release_smoke.py:41-121)
- python scripts/catalog_live_smoke.py --output JSON; exit 1 on FAIL (catalog_live_smoke.py:26-70)
- .\\scripts\\backup_system.ps1 [-BackupPath] [-EnvFile] [-SupabaseUrl] [-ServiceRoleKey SecureString]
- .\\scripts\\scheduled_backup.ps1 [-PassphraseFile] [-BackupRoot] [-KeepLast 0] [-SkipRecording]
- .\\scripts\\register_backup_task.ps1 [-TaskName] [-Time 02:00] [-PassphraseFile] [-BackupRoot] [-KeepLast 14] [-AccountPassword SecureString] [-AllowLoggedInOnly]
- .\\scripts\\record_backup_run.ps1 -BackupDirectory D [-BackupId] [-EnvFile] [-SupabaseUrl] [-ServiceRoleKey]
- .\\scripts\\check_backup_freshness.ps1 [-BackupRoot] [-MaxAgeHours 48] -> exit 0/1
- .\\scripts\\restore_database_local.ps1 -BackupPath P -LocalDatabaseUrl SecureString
- .\\scripts\\restore_storage_local.ps1 -BackupPath P -LocalSupabaseUrl U -LocalServiceRoleKey SecureString
- .\\scripts\\verify-pi03-container-security.ps1 [-ApiImage] [-WorkerImage] [-FrontendImage] [-TrivyImage aquasec/trivy:0.72.0]
- .\\scripts\\verify-pi03-dependencies.ps1 [-BackendImage thesis-v1-api:latest]
- .\\scripts\\verify-pi03-multiarch.ps1
- .\\scripts\\verify-pi03-gemini.ps1
- .\\scripts\\verify-pi04-live.ps1

### Invariants

- release_fingerprint.build_manifest() must stay in agreement with evaluation/run_comparison.py's _ANSWERING_CONFIGURATION_KEYS (run_comparison.py:415-419); adding a key there without adding it to the manifest silently drops a checkpoint guard.
- schema_version must move whenever the manifest's shape changes — release_fingerprint.py:74-91 documents versions 2, 3 and 4, and tests/test_release_fingerprint.py:60 pins 4.
- The manifest must never contain a credential: gateway_host() returns only urlsplit(...).hostname, and tests/test_release_fingerprint.py:85-96 asserts the API key, embedded userinfo and query string are all absent.
- services/prompts.py must remain in the input_sha256 set — tests/test_prompt_contracts.py:86-89 asserts the key 'rag-thesis-backend/services/prompts.py' is present.
- render_openapi() must be byte-identical to docs/evidence/contracts/iskai-openapi.current.json (tests/test_export_openapi.py:27-45) and DuplicationAlert must never gain matched_abstract/matched_excerpt (test_export_openapi.py:48-53).
- objective2Summary.json must equal build() byte-for-byte, be LF-terminated, and keep exactly one stratum flagged quoted with the 'present' stratum non-significant at n=16 (tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py:45-118).
- export_objective2_summary refuses to write unless evaluation/results/checkpoints/<RUN_ID>.provenance.json exists (export_objective2_summary.py:132-135) — a plausible-but-unbacked provenance string must never reach the UI.
- corpus_manifest: EXPECTED_PAPER_COUNT is 12 and is a paper change as well as a code change (corpus_manifest.py:21-36). A lock-ready manifest must hold exactly 12 papers, four completed approvals, unique record_id/paper_id/source_sha256, and unpaid_gemini_eligible === true on every record.
- corpus_manifest lock is write-once: both outputs use open(mode='x'), the two paths must differ, and a failed receipt write unlinks the manifest (corpus_manifest.py:344-394).
- The lock receipt digest is over canonical JSON (compact separators, sorted keys, ensure_ascii=False), not over the file bytes (corpus_manifest.py:70-82).
- reindex_citations: activation is always the last operation, so the previous active index survives any staging failure (comment at reindex_citations.py:240-241). Staged rollback only ever deletes the inactive staged version.
- reindex_citations: a resumed run must belong to the same index configuration — state['index_fingerprint'] is compared against current_index_fingerprint() and mismatch aborts (reindex_citations.py:271-275).
- reindex_citations exit code 0 means every targeted paper succeeded (reindex_citations.py:335-337).
- repair_chunk_sections writes nothing unless the re-derived chunk count matches and every stored content reproduces byte for byte (repair_chunk_sections.py:89-96); embeddings, offsets and page ranges are never touched.
- rescan_duplication / novelty.store_rescan must never overwrite the at-upload duplication_scan keys — the refreshed screen is nested under 'rescan' (novelty.py:416-431, rescan_duplication.py:58-68).
- backfill_screening_archive_size never writes archive_size; only archive_size_estimated, and only where archive_size is None (backfill_screening_archive_size.py:66-77).
- backfill_legacy_locations refuses to apply unless every chunk clears minimum coverage (default 0.70), page_starts are monotonic in chunk order, and every chunk found a 6-word PDF anchor (backfill_legacy_locations.py:129-146).
- build_redacted_derivatives is fail-closed on OCR twice over: globally on OCR_AVAILABLE and per-file on unresolved_scanned_pages.
- seed_synthetic_corpus must prove the target is disposable on four counts before any write: --project-ref equals TEST_SUPABASE_PROJECT_REF, the ref matches the URL hostname label, and both the URL and the service key differ from the application's (seed_synthetic_corpus.py:392-418).
- seed_synthetic_corpus commits only through the production commit_paper_ingestion RPC and chunks/embeds with the real services (module docstring 22-29); a hand-rolled three-table insert is explicitly rejected.
- seed_synthetic_corpus keeps the RNG stream identical across resumed runs by generating text, authors and year before the already-seeded skip check (seed_synthetic_corpus.py:511-539).
- storage_backup restore-local refuses any non-localhost host before create_client is called (storage_backup.py:207-210) — tests/test_operations_security.py:231-239 monkeypatches create_client to fail the test if it is reached.
- storage_backup object paths can never escape objects/<bucket>/ (_object_file, storage_backup.py:103-116) and extraction uses tarfile filter='data'.
- storage_backup manifests reject duplicate (bucket, path) keys and any object whose SHA-256 does not match (_verify_tree, storage_backup.py:124-140).
- A backup folder without sha256-manifest.json is a failed run: scheduled_backup.ps1:39-42 throws, record_backup_run.ps1:33-36 refuses to record it, check_backup_freshness.ps1:16-19 will not count it.
- register_backup_task decides unattended vs InteractiveToken on the decrypted password length, not on SecureString truthiness (register_backup_task.ps1:74-78), because Read-Host -AsSecureString returns a truthy empty object.
- verify-pi03-dependencies only accepts an npm audit response that actually carries metadata.vulnerabilities; an error payload with exit 1 is a failed audit, not a clean tree (verify-pi03-dependencies.ps1:41-49,92-94).
- verify-pi03-multiarch re-hashes every OCI blob before parsing it (Read-OciBlob, verify-pi03-multiarch.ps1:23-34) and requires both linux/amd64 and linux/arm64.
- verify-pi03-gemini asserts the deployed model set from the report, never from script constants — gemini-3.6-flash / gemini-3.5-flash-lite / models/gemini-embedding-001 / 768 (verify-pi03-gemini.ps1:44-54).
- Every verifier's SHA256SUMS.txt excludes itself and is written as ASCII (all five root verifiers).

### Data model

- papers: read by reindex_citations (id,title,authors,year,track,department,filename,storage_path,active_index_version,chunk_count), repair_chunk_sections (id,title,filename,storage_path,active_index_version), rescan_duplication (id,title,department,duplication_scan), backfill_screening_archive_size (id,title,department,created_at,duplication_scan). Written by seed_synthetic_corpus (via commit_paper_ingestion) and by backfill_screening_archive_size (duplication_scan JSONB merge).
- paper_index_versions: inserted and read back by reindex_citations.apply_paper; the row must equal services.index_provenance.current_index_fingerprint() exactly (embedding_model, embedding_dimensions, preprocessing_version, chunking_version, tokenizer, chunk_size_tokens, chunk_overlap_tokens, provenance_status).
- chunks: inserted in 100-row batches by reindex_citations; chunks.section updated by repair_chunk_sections; chunks.metadata updated by backfill_legacy_locations; embeddings read (not written) by rescan_duplication via services.novelty.
- papers.duplication_scan JSONB: at-upload keys at the top level, refreshed screen nested under 'rescan' (novelty.store_rescan), estimated size under 'archive_size_estimated' (backfill_screening_archive_size).
- RPCs called: commit_paper_ingestion (seed_synthetic_corpus.py:603), activate_paper_index (reindex_citations.py:235), prune_inactive_indexes (reindex_citations.py:303; defined at supabase_setup.sql:1039 and last replaced by migrations/20260720_index_embedding_provenance.sql:344), record_backup_run (record_backup_run.ps1:108; migrations/20260804_backup_runs.sql:47), match_chunks (indirectly through services.novelty for the ingest-time screen only).
- Storage buckets: pdfs (private, downloaded by reindex_citations and repair_chunk_sections; backed up), avatars (public, backed up). storage_backup.BUCKETS is the whole set it knows about.
- Release manifest (schema_version 4): {schema_version, git_commit, runtime{python, python_implementation}, models{chat,verdict,embedding}, prompt_version, generation_contract{timeout_seconds,max_retries,max_output_tokens,thinking_level}, generation_route{gateway_enabled,gateway_host,gateway_max_output_tokens,gateway_reasoning_effort,reserve_key_count}, rag_contract{chunk_size_tokens,chunk_overlap_tokens,retrieval_threshold,retrieval_match_count,retrieval_candidate_pool,retrieval_per_paper_cap,duplication_threshold,evaluation_department}, index_fingerprint, input_sha256{11 paths}}.
- Corpus manifest (schema_version 1): {corpus_id, department, expected_paper_count, purpose, status draft|approved|locked, locked_at, processing_profile{gemini_service_tier,allowed_content}, released_composition{released_on,note,represented[],unrepresented[]}, approvals{4 roles x {document_id,approver_name,approver_role,approved_at,document_sha256}}, papers[12] x {record_id,paper_id,title,authors[],year,program,specialization,selection_basis,rights_approval_document_id,privacy_review_document_id,source_sha256,redacted_sha256,index_fingerprint_sha256,unpaid_gemini_eligible}}. Lock receipt: {schema_version,corpus_id,manifest_file,manifest_sha256,paper_count,locked_at}.
- Storage backup manifest: {format:1, created_at, objects:[{bucket,path,size,sha256}]}. Encrypted container: MAGIC b'ISU-STORAGE1' || 16-byte salt || 12-byte nonce || ciphertext || 16-byte GCM tag.
- objective2Summary.json: {_generated_by,_do_not_edit, run{id,date,generated_at,git_commit,prompt_version,formal_result,queries_scored,queries_total,department,framework,judge_model,answer_model,metric,evidence_path}, quoted_stratum, strata[5] x {key,label,baseline,rag,n,delta,p_value,significant,cohens_d_z,ci_95,quoted}, diagnostics{name:{mean,n}}}.
- Redaction receipt sheet: {generated_at, ocr_available, derivative_count, total_embedded_tokens, note, derivatives[] x {record_id,source_filename,source_sha256,redacted_sha256,embedded_payload_sha256,pages_retained,derivative_characters,embedded_chunks,embedded_tokens,redaction_stats,derivative_file}}.
- reindex state file (.reindex_items_9_16_state.json, gitignored at .gitignore:28): {index_fingerprint, completed:[paper_id], failed:{paper_id: message}}.

### Config knobs

- SUPABASE_URL / SUPABASE_KEY (service-role) — read from rag-thesis-backend/.env by config.settings and, independently, by five different ad-hoc parsers in the PowerShell and seeding scripts
- SUPABASE_BACKUP_KEY — service-role key handed to storage_backup.py by backup_system.ps1 and restore_storage_local.ps1; scrubbed from the environment in their finally blocks
- BACKUP_PASSPHRASE — bypasses the interactive prompt and the confirm step in storage_backup._passphrase (storage_backup.py:86-91); minimum 12 characters; set by scheduled_backup.ps1 from a DPAPI file
- GEMINI_API_KEY and the reserve pool (settings.gemini_reserve_key_list, config.py:209) — verify_key_pool checks all of them; release_fingerprint records only the count
- LLM_BASE_URL / LLM_API_KEY / LLM_GATEWAY_MAX_OUTPUT_TOKENS — decide generation_route in the fingerprint; the host is recorded, the credential never is
- GEMINI_CHAT_MODEL / GEMINI_VERDICT_MODEL / GEMINI_EMBED_MODEL / EMBEDDING_DIMENSIONS — asserted literally by verify-pi03-gemini.ps1:44-49
- APP_ENVIRONMENT — forced to 'development' by seed_synthetic_corpus.py:449 and overridden to 'development' by verify-pi03-gemini.ps1:30 to get past config.validate_production_services
- TEST_SUPABASE_URL / TEST_SUPABASE_PROJECT_REF / TEST_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY and ALLOW_DISPOSABLE_SUPABASE_TESTS=1 — the disposable-project gate for seed_synthetic_corpus and verify-pi04-live.ps1
- THESIS_EVALUATION_DEPARTMENT — default department for seed_synthetic_corpus and a recorded rag_contract field
- seed_synthetic_corpus --seed (default 20260804), --count (1..15), --department, --dry-run
- reindex_citations --apply / --allow-model-change / --resume / --prune-old / --older-than-days (default 7) / --fixture-dir
- backfill_legacy_locations --minimum-coverage (default 0.70)
- check_backup_freshness -MaxAgeHours (default 48); scheduled_backup -KeepLast (0 = keep everything) and -SkipRecording; register_backup_task -Time (02:00), -KeepLast (14), -AllowLoggedInOnly
- verify-pi03-container-security -TrivyImage (pinned aquasec/trivy:0.72.0), -ApiImage/-WorkerImage/-FrontendImage; verify-pi03-dependencies -BackendImage
- PUBLIC_API_ORIGIN / VITE_SUPABASE_URL / VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY / VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY — read from the repo-root .env and passed as frontend build args by verify-pi03-multiarch.ps1

### Error handling

- Dry-run-by-default is the dominant safety pattern: reindex_citations, backfill_legacy_locations, backfill_screening_archive_size, repair_chunk_sections and rescan_duplication all require an explicit --apply, and reindex_citations additionally defers the Gemini and Supabase imports into the apply path so a dry run provably makes zero external calls (reindex_citations.py:180, 331).
- Per-item isolation with a non-zero aggregate exit only where it matters: reindex_citations records per-paper failures in state['failed'] and exits 1 if any remain (335-337); repair_chunk_sections and rescan_duplication report per-paper 'error' rows and always exit 0.
- corpus_manifest raises ManifestError for every failure and main() prints it and returns 1 (corpus_manifest.py:454-456); validation accumulates all errors into one message rather than failing on the first.
- build_redacted_derivatives raises DerivativeError and main() returns 1; the OCR gate and the --force overwrite guard are both DerivativeError paths.
- seed_synthetic_corpus sys.exit(2) on a refused target (seed_synthetic_corpus.py:417) and on a bad --count; an embedding failure mid-run breaks the loop and keeps what already committed (548-559), while a commit failure returns 1 immediately.
- Live smokes map to exit codes: gemini_release_smoke, catalog_live_smoke and verify_key_pool all return 0 only when every check passes, and each writes its JSON report regardless so a failure is still evidence.
- storage_backup surfaces integrity failures as ValueError: incomplete container, wrong magic, unexpected EOF, bad manifest format, duplicate object, failed object digest, unsafe object path, unknown bucket, rejected restore host. Nothing is caught — the process exits non-zero.
- PowerShell verifiers use $ErrorActionPreference='Stop' with deliberate 'Continue' windows around native-command invocations so $LASTEXITCODE can be captured, then throw with a message naming the artifact to read (verify-pi03-container-security.ps1:34-41,58-64; verify-pi03-dependencies.ps1:12-13,81; verify-pi03-gemini.ps1:11-18,28-37).
- verify-pi03-dependencies distinguishes 'audit found nothing' from 'audit did not run': a missing pip-audit report, a pip-audit report with no dependencies block, or an npm response without metadata.vulnerabilities after three attempts all throw with an explicit 'was NOT audited' message (lines 83-94).
- scheduled_backup.ps1 treats a missing sha256-manifest.json as a failed run (throws) but treats a failed record_backup_run as a warning only (lines 39-56), on the argument that the staleness alert will catch it.
- verify-pi03-multiarch prints the last 40 build-log lines before throwing and always removes its scratch directory in a finally block (lines 77-84, 185-188).
- verify_key_pool.classify maps provider errors to operator-actionable names — quota_exhausted (via services.chat_notices.is_capacity_error), invalid_key, api_not_enabled, else the exception class name (verify_key_pool.py:43-52).

### Tests that pin it

- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_release_fingerprint.py — pins schema_version 4, the model defaults (gemini-3.6-flash, gemini-3.5-flash-lite, timeout 60.0), the full generation_contract and rag_contract shapes, 768 dimensions, that every input_sha256 value is 64 hex chars, that a gateway changes generation_route, and that no credential, userinfo or query string reaches the manifest.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_prompt_contracts.py:82-90 — asserts manifest['prompt_version'] == prompts.PROMPT_VERSION and that services/prompts.py is present in input_sha256.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_export_openapi.py — determinism, presence of the three /catalog paths, byte-equality with docs/evidence/contracts/iskai-openapi.current.json, and that DuplicationAlert stays metadata-only.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py — committed JSON equals build(), LF-only with a trailing newline, run id/date/formal_result/40 queries/ragas==0.4.3, pooled 0.2169->0.3024 p=3.222e-05 significant, 'present' stratum +0.0601 p=0.1257 n=16 not significant with a CI spanning zero, exactly one quoted stratum, five strata with labels, and the n=4/n=3 strata retained. Module-level skipif when the source run or the output is absent.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_corpus_manifest.py (253 lines) — imports validate/lock/verify and exercises the schema, approvals, composition and immutability rules.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_migration_and_reindex.py (633 lines) — the heaviest script test. Covers reindex_citations dry-run over fixtures (line 128), apply_paper staging and rollback (160), --allow-model-change refusal (198-199), the --allow-model-change-without-apply guard (206-207), the already-compatible skip (240), and the resume fingerprint mismatch (248-256); plus repair_chunk_sections (313-364, 590), rescan_duplication (436-483) and backfill_screening_archive_size (512-538).
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_legacy_location_backfill.py — align_chunks page/section/anchor behaviour and the low-confidence rejection in validate_alignments.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_operations_security.py — storage_backup encryption round trip and wrong-passphrase failure (219-229), restore rejecting a non-local target before create_client is created (231-239), object-path traversal rejection (240-243), duplicate/malformed manifest rejection (245-265), a full backup->verify->restore-local cycle against a fake storage client (910-936), and a source-text assertion over backup_system.ps1 (287-293) checking it reads the .env values, never prompts for the key, uses the stamp format and unsets SUPABASE_BACKUP_KEY.
- Untested: seed_synthetic_corpus.py, build_redacted_derivatives.py, verify_key_pool.py, catalog_live_smoke.py, gemini_release_smoke.py, corpus_manifest's CLI entrypoint, and all twelve PowerShell scripts apart from the backup_system.ps1 text assertion.

### Gotchas

- Only scripts/gemini_release_smoke.py is copied into the container image (rag-thesis-backend/Dockerfile:61). build_redacted_derivatives.py's own docstring (lines 31-32) tells the operator to 'Run it in the same environment that will ingest -- the container, not a Windows host', which is impossible as the image is built. Combined with its OCR_AVAILABLE gate (build_redacted_derivatives.py:110-115) and the fact that the local .venv has no tesserocr (docs/defense/FINAL_DEFENSE_PLAN.md:266-267), the PI-08 derivative step has no environment it can actually run in.
- verify-pi03-dependencies.ps1 cannot work against the current image. Its in-container audit shells `sys.executable -m pip install pip-audit` (verify-pi03-dependencies.ps1:29-36), but Dockerfile:51 runs `pip uninstall --yes pip setuptools` in the build stage and the runtime stage copies that venv, so `python -m pip` is ModuleNotFoundError. The uninstall landed in commit bbf5035 on 2026-08-02; the only committed dependency evidence is docs/evidence/security/pi-03-dependencies-20260725-045627/, dated 2026-07-25 — i.e. the verifier has not produced evidence since the image lost pip.
- scripts/ is outside both quality gates. The lint target is `pylint ... routers services dependencies workers main.py config.py models.py` (.github/workflows/quality.yml:75) and coverage is `--cov=routers --cov=services --cov=dependencies --cov=workers --cov=main --cov=config --cov=models` (quality.yml:70-72). Nothing in scripts/ is linted or counted, even though tests import eight of these modules. .pylintrc's `ignore=venv,.venv,tests` is unrelated — scripts is simply never passed.
- No CI workflow invokes any script in either directory (grep over .github/ finds zero hits). Every evidence artifact under docs/evidence/ is produced by hand, so a stale artifact looks identical to a current one apart from its directory stamp.
- CLAUDE.md states that release_fingerprint.py hashes services/prompts.py, services/chunker.py and services/index_provenance.py. It hashes prompts.py only (release_fingerprint.py:51-72). chunker.py and index_provenance.py reach the manifest solely as constants inside index_fingerprint (CHUNKING_VERSION, TOKENIZER_ENCODING, PREPROCESSING_VERSION), so a behavioural change to chunker.py or index_provenance.py without a version bump leaves the fingerprint byte-identical. services/citations.py, services/embedder.py, services/novelty.py, services/guards.py and services/document_processor.py are likewise unhashed — the citations gap is already recorded at docs/CODEBASE_SECURITY_AUDIT_2026-09-12.md:96.
- Several scripts write JSON with Path.write_text(..., encoding='utf-8') and no newline=' ', so on Windows every becomes \r against a repo that is LF-everywhere by .gitattributes: release_fingerprint.py:151, catalog_live_smoke.py:68, gemini_release_smoke.py:119, verify_key_pool.py:111, storage_backup.py:182, reindex_citations.py:66. export_openapi.py:25, corpus_manifest.py:347 and export_objective2_summary.py:141 do it correctly, which is what makes the inconsistency visible.
- rag-thesis-backend/evaluation/results/reindex_dry_run.json is committed as UTF-16LE with a BOM — a PowerShell 5.1 `>` redirect artifact, not what the script emits.
- storage_backup.BUCKETS is hardcoded to ('pdfs','avatars') (storage_backup.py:25). Any bucket added later is silently excluded from the backup with no warning; docs/defense/FINAL_DEFENSE_PLAN.md:270 already flags a planned `signatures` bucket as an unhandled follow-up.
- seed_synthetic_corpus's disposable-target guard reads only the .env file, never os.environ (resolve_target, seed_synthetic_corpus.py:392-413). If SUPABASE_KEY is supplied as an environment variable rather than in .env, app_key is '' and the 'the disposable service key equals the application SUPABASE_KEY' check passes vacuously. The same is true of the URL comparison.
- seed_synthetic_corpus writes papers with storage_path 'synthetic/<slug>.pdf' (seed_synthetic_corpus.py:574) but never uploads a file. Any later script that downloads by storage_path fails on those rows: repair_chunk_sections returns status 'error' per paper (repair_chunk_sections.py:86-87) and reindex_citations records a failure and exits 1 (reindex_citations.py:175, 296).
- corpus_manifest.PROGRAM_SPECIALIZATIONS (corpus_manifest.py:43-49) mixes naming schemes: BSCS maps to the human track name {'Data Mining'} while BSIT maps to codes {'WMAD','NETSEC'}. services/catalog.py:8-10 is the mapping ('Data Mining' -> ('BSCS','DM')) and catalog_live_smoke.py:23 expects codes {'DM','WMAD','NETSEC'}. A manifest written with 'DM' for BSCS is rejected; one written with 'Web Development' for BSIT is also rejected.
- released_composition is optional: _validate_released_composition returns immediately when the key is absent (corpus_manifest.py:192-193), so a manifest with no composition block can still be locked with no distribution check at all.
- The lock receipt pins the canonical digest, not the file bytes. Reformatting the locked manifest's whitespace or key order leaves `verify` passing.
- reindex_citations.apply_paper swallows the exception when activation already landed (reindex_citations.py:239-250): the `raise` is inside `if active_version != staged_version`, so if activate_paper_index succeeded and something downstream threw, the function falls through to the success return.
- reindex_citations.load_state returns a blank state on OSError or JSONDecodeError (reindex_citations.py:60-62), so a corrupted checkpoint makes --resume silently restart from zero instead of failing. Separately, save_state is called on every iteration even without --resume (line 299), so a non-resume run clobbers any existing state file.
- reindex_citations.apply_paper is annotated `-> dict` but returns None for an already-compatible index (line 170); run_apply relies on that None to classify a skip.
- fetch_papers filters ingestion_status='ready' only on the --all path (reindex_citations.py:144-145); --paper-id will happily re-index a failed or in-flight paper.
- backfill_legacy_locations writes page/section into chunks.metadata only (backfill_legacy_locations.py:170-177), never into the chunks.page_start/page_end/section columns. It works because retriever.py:104-106 falls back to metadata, but the two writers are now inconsistent: reindex_citations writes both, repair_chunk_sections writes only the column.
- backfill_screening_archive_size does a read-modify-write of the duplication_scan JSONB with no version check (backfill_screening_archive_size.py:91-93). Running it concurrently with an ingest — services/novelty.refresh_after_ingest now rewrites other papers' screenings at ingest time — loses whichever write lands first.
- rescan_duplication --apply calls store_rescan for every paper it processed, but the closing line reports only the count that changed (rescan_duplication.py:84-85, 110-111): 'rewrote 3 changed screening(s) of 16 paper(s)' understates that 16 rows were written.
- repair_chunk_sections selects chunks with .eq('index_version', active_index) even when active_index_version is None (repair_chunk_sections.py:76-79), which PostgREST turns into index_version=eq.None and returns nothing — the paper is reported as 'no chunks' rather than as a provenance problem.
- restore_database_local.ps1:17 builds the container URI with a naive `.Replace('localhost','host.docker.internal').Replace('127.0.0.1','host.docker.internal')` over the whole connection string, so those substrings inside a password or database name are rewritten too.
- restore_storage_local.ps1 never sets BACKUP_PASSPHRASE, so storage_backup._passphrase() prompts interactively twice — once for `verify` (line 17) and again for `restore-local` (line 21).
- scheduled_backup.ps1's pruning (lines 58-67) deletes every directory matching ^\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}-\d{6}$ beyond -KeepLast regardless of whether it holds a sha256-manifest.json, so an incomplete run can push a good one out of retention. It is also the only destructive step in the backup path.
- backup_system.ps1 depends on a locally linked Supabase CLI project: `npx.cmd supabase db dump` is run from the repository root with no --db-url, and supabase/ is gitignored (.gitignore:75) holding only .temp/linked-project.json. On a fresh clone the three dump calls fail or prompt. The script also hard-rejects any URL that is not *.supabase.co (line 48), so it cannot back up a self-hosted instance.
- backup_system.ps1's manifest classifier (line 88) labels anything that is not .sql as 'encrypted_storage_artifact', which mislabels storage.isubackup.report.json (written by storage_backup.py:182). ConvertTo-Json on a single-element collection also emits an object rather than an array.
- record_backup_run.ps1 sends the service-role key as both apikey and Bearer directly from PowerShell (lines 108-113). Setting $plainServiceKey = $null afterwards does not scrub the already-materialised .NET string.
- The four .env parsers disagree. backup_system.ps1:19-38 and record_backup_run.ps1:38-57 return the FIRST match; verify-pi04-live.ps1:10-16 returns the LAST (`Select-Object -Last 1`); verify-pi03-multiarch.ps1:11-21 builds a whole hashtable with a different regex; seed_synthetic_corpus.read_env_file (Python, lines 379-389) is a fifth. A duplicated key in .env resolves differently depending on which tool reads it.
- verify-pi03-dependencies.ps1 applies asymmetric thresholds: every pip-audit finding of any severity fails (line 96-100), but only npm High+Critical counts (lines 101-103).
- verify-pi03-multiarch.ps1 reads the repo-root .env and passes VITE_SUPABASE_URL / VITE_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY / VITE_TURNSTILE_SITE_KEY / PUBLIC_API_ORIGIN as --build-arg (lines 121-127), baking them into image history, and writes the full buildx log into the same evidence directory it then SHA-256s.
- verify-pi03-container-security.ps1 bind-mounts /var/run/docker.sock into the Trivy container (line 50) — a host-root-equivalent mount on a Linux daemon — and builds the evidence mount string from a Windows path with backslashes.
- catalog_live_smoke.py is described as 'Live ... API verification' and the generated SUMMARY.md calls it 'PI-04 Live Catalog API Verification', but it uses fastapi.testclient.TestClient(app) in-process (catalog_live_smoke.py:31). What is live is the Supabase project behind it, not the HTTP surface.
- verify_key_pool.py spends real free-tier quota on every run (one chat invoke per key) and returns exit 1 when GEMINI_API_KEY is unset, which reads as a failed check rather than a skipped one.
- tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py:30-33 skips the entire module when either the pinned comparison run or the generated summary is missing, so deleting evaluation/results/comparison_20260913_064053.json makes the pin disappear silently instead of failing.
- release_fingerprint.py, export_openapi.py, export_objective2_summary.py and build_redacted_derivatives.py have no sys.path bootstrap (unlike catalog_live_smoke.py:15-17, verify_key_pool.py:28, gemini_release_smoke.py:11, reindex_citations.py:17-19 and both backfills), so they must be run as `python -m scripts.X` from rag-thesis-backend/. build_redacted_derivatives additionally defaults to the relative path evaluation/corpus/private (line 44), making it cwd-dependent.
- tests/test_dockerfile_contents.py resolves only the imports of main.py and workers/ingestion_worker.py; it does not cover the scripts/ COPY line, so gemini_release_smoke.py's presence in the image is unpinned and any new in-image script would be missed.
- docs/FIDELITY_AUDIT_2026-09-02.html:255 and :397 still describe corpus_manifest.py as enforcing a 50-paper corpus; the constant has been 12 since the 2026-09-07 release (corpus_manifest.py:21-36).

### Open questions

- Has verify-pi03-dependencies.ps1 been run successfully since 2026-08-02 (commit bbf5035, which removed pip from the venv)? Its npm retry logic was authored 2026-09-04 per its own comment, yet no evidence directory exists for that date — so either the pip-audit step failed silently ahead of the npm work or the run was abandoned before writing evidence.
- How is build_redacted_derivatives.py meant to be executed given it is not in the image (Dockerfile:61 copies only gemini_release_smoke.py) and it refuses to run without OCR_AVAILABLE? Is there an intended fourth COPY line, or a separate tooling image?
- Should release_fingerprint.py's input_sha256 be widened to services/chunker.py, services/index_provenance.py, services/citations.py, services/embedder.py and services/novelty.py? CLAUDE.md already claims two of these are hashed, and the schema_version comment block is the established place to record such a change.
- Was scripts/ intentionally excluded from the pylint and coverage gates, or is that an omission? Eight of these modules are imported by the test suite, so raising them into --cov would shift the 85% denominator.
- corpus_manifest.PROGRAM_SPECIALIZATIONS: should BSCS use 'DM' (the catalog code) rather than 'Data Mining' (the legacy track label), and is the currently-locked/working manifest written against the label?
- Does any locked corpus manifest or lock receipt actually exist under evaluation/corpus/private/ (gitignored), and has verify been run against it? Only the empty template is committed.
- storage_backup.BUCKETS: is the planned signatures bucket (FINAL_DEFENSE_PLAN.md:270) going to be added, and should BUCKETS instead be discovered from storage.buckets so a future bucket cannot be silently omitted?
- seed_synthetic_corpus writes storage_path values with no file behind them; should the seeder also upload a placeholder PDF so the repair and reindex scripts do not error on synthetic rows, or should those scripts learn to skip TITLE_MARKER papers?

**Depends on:** config.settings (every Python script except corpus_manifest.py and export_openapi.py), services.index_provenance.current_index_fingerprint / is_embedding_compatible, services.prompts.PROMPT_VERSION, services.gemini_pool.gateway_enabled, services.chunker (split_document, build_chunk_metadata, validate_chunk_records, record_overlap_tokens, count_tokens, _is_section_heading — a private import in backfill_legacy_locations.py:20), services.document_processor (extract_document, is_noise_chunk, OCR_AVAILABLE, ExtractedDocument/ExtractedPage), services.embedder.embed_texts (apply paths only), services.novelty (percent, rescan_indexed_paper, store_rescan), services.retriever.sb (the shared service-role Supabase client used by every data-plane script), services.chat_notices.is_capacity_error (verify_key_pool), main.app (export_openapi, catalog_live_smoke), langchain_google_genai, supabase-py, cryptography (scrypt + AES-GCM), fastapi.testclient, External binaries: git, docker / docker compose / docker buildx, tar.exe, npx.cmd + the Supabase CLI (with a linked project under the gitignored supabase/), npm.cmd, psql inside postgres:17-alpine, aquasec/trivy:0.72.0, docker-compose.operations.yml (verify-pi03-gemini), rag-thesis-backend/Dockerfile and rag-thesis-frontend/Dockerfile (verify-pi03-multiarch)

**Used by:** rag-thesis-backend/evaluation/run_comparison.py:69,423,898 — imports build_manifest and sha256_file; the manifest both gates checkpoint reuse and is published as reproducibility.release in every comparison JSON, rag-thesis-frontend/src/data/objective2Summary.json — generated by export_objective2_summary.py and rendered by the admin Overview card (AdminOverview.jsx, modified in the working tree), docs/evidence/contracts/iskai-openapi.current.json — generated by export_openapi.py, diffed by tests/test_export_openapi.py, docs/evidence/security/pi-03-*, pi-03-dependencies-*, pi-03-gemini-*, pi-03-multiarch-* and docs/evidence/pi-04/live-* — produced by the five repo-root verifiers; indexed in docs/evidence/PI_VERIFICATION_2026-07-25.md:61-64, docs/OPERATIONS_SECURITY_RUNBOOK.md:43,63,67,72,80,83 — the operator procedure for the backup and restore scripts, docs/governance/PI08_APPROVAL_PRIVACY_CORPUS_PROTOCOL.md:115-138 — the validate/lock/verify command sequence for corpus_manifest, README.md:62-64 — reindex_citations --apply --all --allow-model-change as the documented embedding-model change procedure; README.md:412 — release_fingerprint for generation_route, docs/defense/FINAL_DEFENSE_PLAN.md:44-45, 267-270, 290, 337 — export_openapi, rescan_duplication + backfill_screening_archive_size after each bulk tranche, the flagged signatures-bucket backup gap, and verify_key_pool on defense morning, The Supabase operations monitor: record_backup_run.ps1 feeds the backup_stale alert condition (migrations/20260804_backup_runs.sql)

**Additional notes returned by the reader** (fields outside the schema, preserved verbatim)

- `controlFlow_unused`: 

**Adversarial fact-check:** verdict `mostly-accurate`, 16 correction(s), 9 omission(s). Corrections are already folded into the text above. They are kept because each records a wrong belief a competent reader reached from the same files - the same trap is available to you.
