- `rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_network_retry.py` (54 lines) - 3 tests. WinError 10035 retried once with a 0.25 s delay, non-transient never retried, transient bounded by `attempts`.
- `rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_export_openapi.py` (53 lines) - 3 tests but the strongest drift gate in the repo: byte-equality between render_openapi() and docs/evidence/contracts/iskai-openapi.current.json (:27), determinism, and DuplicationAlert schema without matched_abstract/matched_excerpt.
- `rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_evaluation_exports.py` (62 lines) - 3 tests. LangSmith export carries metrics but never inputs/outputs and raises on payload content; Sonar export uses the component-measure API rather than /api/hotspots/search.
- `rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_dockerfile_contents.py` (72 lines) - 3 tests. REPOSITORY-FACT. AST-resolves first-party top-level imports of main.py and workers/ingestion_worker.py and asserts each is covered by a build-stage COPY; plus warning_filters.py named explicitly (:70).
- `rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_legacy_location_backfill.py` (46 lines) - 2 tests. scripts.backfill_legacy_locations align_chunks page/section mapping and the low-confidence rejection at minimum_coverage 0.7.
- `rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_activity.py` (49 lines) - 2 tests. log_activity derives department from profiles and swallows client failures (returns None).
- `rag-thesis-backend/probe7b.py` (45 lines) - NOT a test file, but relevant: a committed scratch script at the backend root that imports tests.test_guards_multilingual and exec()s /tmp/probe6.py. Outside testpaths so pytest never collects it, outside the pylint target list, outside the --cov list, and not in the Dockerfile COPY list — so nothing in the gate touches it.

### Control flow

Collection starts from `pytest.ini` (testpaths=tests, pythonpath=.). Before any test module is imported, `tests/conftest.py` runs three things in order and nothing else — there are no fixtures in conftest at all, so every fixture in the suite is file-local. First it prepends the backend root to `sys.path` (:13) so the suite works from any invocation directory. Second it imports and calls `warning_filters.silence_known_third_party_warnings()` (:15-17), which must happen before the first `langchain*` import; this mirrors what `main.py` and `workers/ingestion_worker.py` do. Third it force-writes 17 environment variables (:19-44) — GEMINI_API_KEY='test-key', GEMINI_API_KEYS='', LLM_BASE_URL='', LLM_API_KEY='', a fake SUPABASE_URL/KEY, APP_ENVIRONMENT=test, RATE_LIMIT_STORAGE_URI=memory://, REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA=false, MALWARE_SCAN_MODE=disabled, OPERATIONS_MONITOR_ENABLED=false, RETENTION_ENFORCEMENT_ENABLED=false, both LangSmith flags false, TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY='', REQUIRE_OCR_FOR_SCANNED_PAGES=true. The comment at :21-25 is load-bearing: routing, not just credentials, is pinned, because four real GEMINI_API_KEYS reddened `test_gemini_pool` rotation assertions locally and a set LLM_BASE_URL put the gateway in front of every monkeypatched `chat.llm`, making twelve tests hit the network. `_warn_unless_pinned_interpreter()` (:47-88) then prints to stderr when `sys.prefix` is not `<backend>/.venv`; it never fails, because CI installs the lock into the job environment with no `.venv`.

Because the environment is frozen at import time, `config.settings` is a single process-wide singleton shared by every test, and nearly every test that needs a different value uses `monkeypatch.setattr(settings, ...)` rather than constructing a new `Settings`. The exceptions are the validation tests (`test_rag_controls.TestServerConfiguration`, `test_worker_concurrency`, `test_operations_security`, `test_index_provenance`), which build `Settings(**BASE)` directly and pass `_env_file: None` where they must not read `.env`.

The suite decomposes into four layers. **Layer 1 is repository-fact**: nine modules read committed non-Python artifacts as text and assert literal substrings — `test_readme_accuracy` (4 Markdown files + package.json + sonar-project.properties), `test_dependency_lock` (requirements.txt/.lock), `test_dockerfile_contents` (Dockerfile, AST-driven), `test_schema_consistency` and `test_migration_and_reindex` and parts of `test_catalog`/`test_thesis_category`/`test_backup_monitoring`/`test_operations_security`/`test_security_upload_hardening`/`test_durable_ingestion` (supabase_setup.sql and named migrations), `test_chat_notices` (two backfill migrations + rollback + base schema), `test_release_fingerprint` and `test_index_provenance` (config defaults reflected through the manifest), `test_corpus_manifest` (PI-08), and `test_evaluation_harness.TestGoldenDatasetStrata` (the shipped 40-query instrument). These fail on drift with no code change. `test_export_openapi.py:27` is the sharpest of them: a byte-for-byte equality between the live FastAPI schema and `docs/evidence/contracts/iskai-openapi.current.json`, so adding a query parameter anywhere breaks it until the contract is regenerated.

**Layer 2 is structural AST/regex auditing of production source.** `test_event_loop_responsiveness.TestNoBlockingCallCreepsBackIn` (:450-492) walks every `async def` in `routers/`, `services/` and `main.py`, collects `ast.Call` nodes not inside an `Await` and not inside an `add_task(...)` argument, and fails if the first line of the call's source segment contains any of 34 known-blocking substrings (`sb.rpc`, `sb.table`, `fitz.open`, `embed_texts`, `resolve_effective_department`, `_persist_chat_exchange`, …). It self-guards twice: :500 asserts at least 15 async functions were inspected, and :510 writes a regressed file into tmp_path and asserts the audit flags it. `test_hardening_fixes.TestExplicitFieldListsInHotPaths` does the same trick twice more — an AST scan forbidding `select('*')` anywhere in production modules, and a SQL parse that builds a table→columns map from every `CREATE TABLE`/`ALTER TABLE ADD COLUMN` in the repo and asserts six pinned SELECT field lists are *equal* to the declared column sets. Both are guarded against becoming vacuous.

**Layer 3 is behavioural unit testing with per-file stub classes.** There is no shared fake Supabase; `grep` finds 45 distinct `Client`/`Query`/`_*Client` stub definitions across the test files, each modelling exactly the PostgREST builder methods that the code under test happens to call (`select`/`eq`/`in_`/`ilike`/`order`/`limit`/`range`/`single`/`insert`/`update`/`delete`/`execute`, plus `rpc` and `storage.from_`). The one place that models PostgREST's `db-max-rows` truncation is `test_router_crud.py:373`, which caps both ranged and unranged reads at 1000 rows and proves `analytics.overview` pages through 2500 papers. Async handlers are driven with bare `asyncio.run(...)` helpers (`run(coro)` defined in at least six files); `slowapi`-decorated endpoints must be reached through `inspect.unwrap(...)` or `.__wrapped__` and, because slowapi's wrapper inspects the argument, several files build a real `starlette.requests.Request` from a raw ASGI scope rather than a `SimpleNamespace` (`public_request()` in test_catalog/test_router_crud/test_hardening_fixes, `request()`/`delete_request()` in test_duplication_flows and test_chat_endpoint_flows). `routers/chat.chat` carries two decorators, so `test_chat_endpoint_flows.py:740` reaches it as `chat.chat.__wrapped__.__wrapped__`.

**Layer 4 is parametrized string corpora.** Three files — `test_guards_multilingual` (323), `test_chat_routing_multilingual` (234) and `test_followup_multilingual` (162) — contribute 719 of the 2,143 tests, 33.5% of the headline count, by parametrizing large frozen lists of Tagalog/Ilocano/Taglish/English question strings against a handful of regex predicates in `services/guards.py` and `routers/chat.py`. Each corpus is paired with a deliberately larger negative corpus, and the files' docstrings state why: refusing a legitimate retrieval question or pinning a standalone question to the previous answer's thesis is worse than a miss. This design is correct for the defect class but means the test count and the coverage figure are dominated by a small number of source lines.

Through all four layers the frozen pipeline is protected by a small set of tripwires that appear in more than one place: 800/100/cl100k_base/token-v1 appear in test_chunker, test_hardening_fixes, test_index_provenance and test_migration_and_reindex; 768 appears in those plus test_release_fingerprint, test_retriever, test_thesis_category and the RLS integration payload; 0.85 appears in test_novelty, test_chat_logic, test_retriever and test_rag_controls. Changing one of them fails in four to eight files at once, which is the intended signal that a paper table has to move with it.

CI (`.github/workflows/quality.yml:70-72`) runs the suite with `--cov=routers --cov=services --cov=dependencies --cov=workers --cov=main --cov=config --cov=models --cov-fail-under=85`, and `.coveragerc` sets `branch = false`, so the gate is line coverage over seven roots. `scripts/`, `evaluation/`, `warning_filters.py` and the stray `probe7b.py` are outside both that measure and the pylint target list at :75, even though roughly 90 tests target `scripts/` and `evaluation/` modules directly.

### Invariants

- conftest must set os.environ BEFORE any test module imports config; `settings` is a process-wide singleton and there is no per-test reset, so any test that mutates it must use monkeypatch.
- warning_filters.silence_known_third_party_warnings() is called in conftest before the first langchain import, matching main.py and workers/ingestion_worker.py. Any conftest reordering that imports a service first changes what warnings the suite sees.
- pytest.ini's filterwarnings names `starlette.exceptions.StarletteDeprecationWarning`. pytest resolves filter categories by import during config parsing, so a Starlette build without that class aborts the entire run before collection. This is the documented failure mode of running outside .venv (conftest.py:52-63).
- The frozen RAG contract: chunk_size_tokens 800, chunk_overlap_tokens 100, embedding_dimensions 768, retrieval_threshold 0.30, retrieval_match_count 5, retrieval_candidate_pool 15, retrieval_per_paper_cap 3, duplication_threshold 0.85. Each is asserted in at least two independent files.
- Model names: gemini-3.6-flash (chat), gemini-3.5-flash-lite (verdict), models/gemini-embedding-001 (embed). Only the first two are pinned as Settings.model_fields defaults (test_release_fingerprint.py:12-13); the embed model is pinned only transitively through manifest['models']['embedding'] == settings.gemini_embed_model, i.e. tautologically.
- Version strings: PROMPT_VERSION 'iskai-prompt-v4', CHUNKING_VERSION 'token-v1', PREPROCESSING_VERSION 'document-v1', TOKENIZER_ENCODING 'cl100k_base', release manifest schema_version 4.
- auth.PRIVILEGED_MFA_REQUIRED_DETAIL is a verbatim contract shared with rag-thesis-frontend/src/lib/privilegedMfa.js: 'Multi-factor authentication is required for privileged access.' All five privileged guards must use it, and no role-only refusal may.
- Every entry in chat_notices.NOTICE_MARKERS must classify as KIND_NOTICE via response_kind, and — unless exempted in MARKERS_WITHOUT_HISTORICAL_ROWS — must appear (first 48 normalized chars, quotes doubled) in the concatenated 20260804 + 20260825 backfill SQL.
- Chat history loading must filter `kind = 'answer'` in SQL, and the filter must be applied textually before `.limit(5)`.
- Both supabase_setup.sql and the newest migration must set hnsw.ef_search above 40 inside match_chunks and check_topic_duplication, must keep the cancel_requested_at guard inside commit_upload_ingestion, and must reference paper_index_versions + provenance inside commit_paper_ingestion.
- The set of functions redefined across migrations is exactly 15 names and every redefinition chain must end at the highest filename, because migrations are applied in filename order and `create or replace function` keeps whichever body ran last.
- requirements.txt and requirements.lock must pin identical versions, every locked package must carry at least one well-formed sha256 hash (pip --require-hashes fails the whole install otherwise), the lock header must record a linux resolve, and cryptography must stay at major >= 50.
- Every first-party top-level import of main.py and workers/ingestion_worker.py must be covered by a Dockerfile COPY; a root-level module must be named literally.
- No async function in routers/, services/ or main.py may call any of the 34 known-blocking helpers outside an await (BackgroundTasks.add_task arguments excepted), and no production module may call select('*').
- The six pinned SELECT field lists must EQUAL — not merely be a subset of — the columns the committed SQL declares for their tables.
- render_openapi() must be byte-identical to docs/evidence/contracts/iskai-openapi.current.json, and DuplicationAlert must never expose matched_abstract or matched_excerpt.
- The golden dataset is exactly 40 queries stratified 16 present / 17 absent_topic / 4 absent_unreleased / 3 absent_by_design, with zero undetermined, negative_control == absent_by_design exactly, and every absent_unreleased question naming 'web development', 'network security' or 'bsdsa' in its own text.
- EXPECTED_PAPER_COUNT == 12 (PI-08 released corpus).
- The disposable-Supabase config guard must pytest.fail (not skip) when pointed at the application project, when the ref does not match the host, when the keys are not valid anon/service_role JWTs for that ref, or when anon == service.

### Data model

- No shared fixtures: `tests/conftest.py` defines none. Every fixture is file-local, and 45 distinct stub `Client`/`Query` classes are defined across the suite.
- Canonical PostgREST stub shape (test_auth_authorization.py:10-35, test_router_crud.py:25-60, test_retriever.py:69-97, test_catalog.py:28-67): a `Query` whose select/eq/in_/ilike/order/limit/range/single/insert/update/delete all return self and whose `execute()` returns `SimpleNamespace(data=..., count=...)`, wrapped in a `Client` with `.table(name)` and `.rpc(name, args)`.
- `RpcClient` (test_operations_security.py:36, test_durable_ingestion.py) records `(name, payload)` tuples so tests can assert the RPC name and argument dict — this is how the p_kind, p_public_error, p_failure_category, p_thesis_category and provenance parameters are pinned.
- `_RpcCapture` (test_thesis_category.py:89-99) asserts the RPC name and captures args, returning empty rows so retrieval short-circuits before any table lookup.
- Storage stubs: `_StorageBucket`/`_Storage`/`_IngestClient` (test_security_upload_hardening.py:97-126) record uploaded (path, file_options) and removed paths, with fail_upload/fail_remove switches for compensation paths.
- LLM stubs: `fake_llm(reply, fail_with)` exposing async `ainvoke` (test_duplication_flows.py:82); `_fake_llm(replies)` cycling a queue and raising Exception instances (test_router_error_paths.py:55); `_capture(monkeypatch, module)` replacing `module.gemini_pool.arun` with a recorder (test_untrusted_prompt_framing.py:30); and `reply(content, finish_reason)` producing `SimpleNamespace(content=..., response_metadata={'finish_reason': ...})` (test_generation_truncation.py:41).
- `FakeStorage` (test_guest_budget.py:22-42) mirrors the two `limits` storage methods (incr with expiry, get) plus a `failures` counter for fail-open testing.
- Real ASGI Request construction from a raw scope dict is used wherever slowapi wraps the handler: `public_request()` / `request()` / `delete_request()` in test_catalog, test_router_crud, test_hardening_fixes, test_duplication_flows, test_chat_endpoint_flows, test_rate_limit.
- `_NoRequest` (a class with `headers = {}`) is the minimal stand-in for `_chat_impl`, which only reads headers; defined in test_chat_endpoint_flows.py:17 and re-imported by test_generation_truncation.py:29.
- Real PDFs are built with PyMuPDF in-process (`fitz.open(); new_page(); insert_text(); tobytes()`) in test_document_processor, test_security_upload_hardening, test_duplication_flows, test_durable_ingestion and test_router_error_paths — the scan path now refuses the old `b'%PDF-dummy'` stand-in.
- `PipelineClient` / `BatchClient` (test_durable_ingestion.py) model the full download→hash→rpc sequence including a `commit_error` + `recovered` pair for the ambiguous-commit recovery path.
- `PagingQuery`/`PagingClient` (test_router_crud.py:373) is the only stub that models PostgREST's db-max-rows truncation, capping both ranged and unranged reads at 1000 rows.

### Config knobs

- ALLOW_DISPOSABLE_SUPABASE_TESTS=1 — unlocks the two @pytest.mark.integration RLS tests; also requires TEST_SUPABASE_URL, TEST_SUPABASE_ANON_KEY, TEST_SUPABASE_SERVICE_ROLE_KEY, TEST_SUPABASE_PROJECT_REF, all cross-validated against the application project before anything is written.
- RUN_CLAMAV_INTEGRATION=1 — unlocks test_operations_security.py:267 (EICAR against a live clamd).
- CI=<any> — suppresses conftest's wrong-interpreter warning (conftest.py:71-72).
- Presence of scipy on the path — enables 5 statistical tests in test_evaluation_harness (importlib.util.find_spec, not an import).
- Existence of evaluation/results/comparison_20260913_064053.json AND rag-thesis-frontend/src/data/objective2Summary.json — enables the 8 Objective 2 export tests; both absent means a silent module-level skip.
- PYTHONHASHSEED — not pinned anywhere in the repo; test_chat_logic.TestGreetingMatchIsDeterministic exists because set iteration order made `hi there friend` match under seeds 0/2/7 and not 1/3. The fix is in production code (_GREETINGS_BY_LENGTH sorted longest-first), not in the environment.
- The 17 env vars conftest force-writes (conftest.py:19-41) — changing any of them changes what the whole suite exercises; notably MALWARE_SCAN_MODE=disabled, REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA=false, TURNSTILE_SECRET_KEY='' and RATE_LIMIT_STORAGE_URI=memory:// mean the hardened production paths are only reached by per-test monkeypatching.

### Error handling

- Assertion messages are written as instructions, not diagnostics: test_dependency_lock.py:67-72 prints the exact `uv pip compile` command; test_schema_consistency.py:148-152 tells the reader to either sync the base file or add a KNOWN_BASE_SCHEMA_DRIFT entry with a reason; test_export_openapi.py:40-45 names the regeneration command in the failure text.
- Guard-the-guard tests are a deliberate pattern: test_readme_accuracy.py:49-57, test_dependency_lock.py:74-80, test_schema_consistency.py:111-130, test_hardening_fixes.py:627 and :675, test_event_loop_responsiveness.py:500 and :510, test_evaluation_harness.py:114-121. Each proves the parser/detector is not silently returning empty.
- HTTP status contracts asserted throughout: 401/403 for unauthenticated protected routes; 403 for role and MFA refusals; 404 for missing-but-owned resources and for malformed uuids read as absent (test_duplication_flows.py:297); 409 for department rename/delete conflicts, duplicate codes and session department mismatch; 413 for oversized bodies and over-cap batches; 415 for wrong MIME; 422 for validation, unknown category, unknown department selection, and malformed PDFs; 502 for generation failure; 503 for database outage, unavailable malware scanner, pre-migration column errors, and the public-summary decline.
- Fail-open vs fail-closed is asserted explicitly in both directions: guest_budget fails OPEN on storage failure (test_guest_budget.py:119-128); turnstile fails CLOSED on network error (test_guest_turnstile.py:108-125); the malware scanner fails CLOSED with 503 in both ingestion and the scan endpoint (test_duplication_flows.py:167-174); an unparseable backup timestamp fails STALE rather than fresh (test_backup_monitoring.py:135-141); an unknown scanner state fails closed (test_operations_security.py:356); the no-evidence sentinel near-miss fails closed (test_prompt_contracts.py:157-160).
- Provider capacity errors are never raised: pinned as a 60 s process-wide cooldown returning HTTP 200 with CAPACITY_MESSAGE (test_chat_endpoint_flows.py:138, test_router_error_paths.py:250 and :264), and the evaluation harness classifies it as unattempted rather than wrong (test_evaluation_harness.py:257-278).
- Truncated generations raise TruncatedGeneration inside the repair helpers and are converted to the grounded fallback by the caller, never served (test_generation_truncation.py:146-210).
- test_router_error_paths.py:67-70 has an autouse fixture that calls chat_notices.reset_capacity_limit() after every test, because the cooldown is process-wide module state.

Total 2143.

Moving or renaming test_chat_endpoint_flows breaks test_generation_truncation.

### Gotchas

- **The `models/gemini-embedding-2` vs `models/gemini-embedding-001` split is real and shipped.** config.py:18 sets gemini_embed_model = 'models/gemini-embedding-001'. But migrations/20260720_index_embedding_provenance.sql:48 and supabase_setup.sql:354 backfill the legacy provenance rows with the literal 'models/gemini-embedding-2', and test_migration_and_reindex.py:96 pins that literal. services/index_provenance.py:37 requires `index['embedding_model'] == settings.gemini_embed_model`, and match_chunks filters on `piv.embedding_model = p_embedding_model` — so every `legacy_assumed` row the backfill writes is permanently unretrievable and permanently incompatible. test_index_provenance.py:31-37 exercises the `legacy_assumed` branch only after substituting the CURRENT model name, and :45-47 uses 'models/gemini-embedding-2' as an example of an INCOMPATIBLE index. Nothing tests the literal the SQL actually writes. The opt-in RLS test compounds it: test_supabase_rls_integration.py:271 commits a paper whose index_provenance names 'models/gemini-embedding-2', so the row it asserts is ingested could never be retrieved.
- **`probe7b.py` is committed scratch at the backend root** (45 lines, in commit 576f59f). It does `import services.guards`, `from tests.test_guards_multilingual import MUST_ALLOW, MUST_REFUSE`, and `exec(open('/tmp/probe6.py').read()...)`. It is outside `testpaths=tests` so pytest never collects it, outside the pylint target list so it is never linted, outside the `--cov` roots so it never counts, and not in the Dockerfile COPY list so test_dockerfile_contents cannot see it. An earlier commit (1c0b762) untracked exactly this shape of file; this one slipped through.
- **Coverage gate scope excludes a lot of tested, defense-critical code.** `--cov` covers only routers/ services/ dependencies/ workers/ main.py config.py models.py. It does NOT cover scripts/ (release_fingerprint, corpus_manifest, reindex_citations, storage_backup, export_openapi, backfill_legacy_locations, repair_chunk_sections, rescan_duplication, backfill_screening_archive_size, export_objective2_summary), evaluation/ (run_comparison, summarize_jmeter, export_langsmith, export_sonar), or warning_filters.py — yet ~90 tests target them. The same list is the pylint target list, so warning_filters.py (imported first by BOTH entrypoints) is neither linted nor measured.
- **Nothing pins the Python version across its three declarations.** Dockerfile:27 asserts `sys.version_info[:3] == (3, 14, 7)`; README.md:74 says 3.14.7; quality.yml:47 pins '3.14.7'. The Dockerfile's own comment instructs the reader to keep the workflow, README and the paper's Tables 1-4 in step — and there is no test doing it. test_dockerfile_contents only checks the COPY list. Grep for '3.14.7' in tests/ returns nothing.
- **The release fingerprint's hashed input list is incomplete and untested for completeness.** scripts/release_fingerprint.py:51-72 hashes requirements.lock, config.py, routers/chat.py, services/prompts.py, services/retriever.py, services/question_types.py, the frontend package-lock, both Dockerfiles and docker-compose.operations.yml. It does NOT hash services/chunker.py, services/index_provenance.py, services/citations.py, services/guards.py or services/query_translation.py. CLAUDE.md states the script 'hashes these files' for prompts/chunker/index_provenance — only prompts.py is actually hashed; the other two reach the manifest solely through their version *constants* inside index_fingerprint, so a chunker logic change without a CHUNKING_VERSION bump leaves the manifest identical. services/citations.py decides whether an answer is served or replaced by the grounded fallback and services/guards.py decides refusal — neither moves the fingerprint. And nothing pins the list: test_prompt_contracts.py:86 asserts only that prompts.py is present, test_release_fingerprint.py:33 only the package-lock entry.
- **The new Objective 2 export is a three-file untracked set and will be silently vacuous in CI.** `git status` shows `?? rag-thesis-backend/scripts/export_objective2_summary.py`, `?? rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py`, `?? rag-thesis-frontend/src/data/`. AdminOverview.jsx:24 already does `import OBJECTIVE2 from '../../data/objective2Summary.json'`, so a clean checkout fails `npm run build`. Meanwhile the test's module-level skipif (:30-33) requires `OUT.exists()`, so if the script and test land without the JSON, all 8 tests skip with no failure. The script's own docstring (:21) says the JSON 'IS committed' — it is not, yet.
- **test_schema_consistency deliberately tolerates two different implementations of the retrieval path.** KNOWN_BASE_SCHEMA_DRIFT (:88-98) records that supabase_setup.sql implements match_chunks and check_topic_duplication as `language plpgsql` while the migration uses `language sql stable`, and only the ef_search setting is cross-checked. A project built from the base file runs a different function body for both frozen retrieval RPCs than a migrated project, and the suite asserts the claim 'both return the same rows' only in a comment.
- **One third of the test count is regex-corpus parametrization.** 719 of 2,143 tests live in three files pinning string lists against `services/guards.py` and a handful of `routers/chat.py` predicates. The headline test count is quoted as evidence in the README and the paper; it is dominated by a small number of source lines. The 85% line-coverage gate is the figure that actually measures breadth, and the two are easy to conflate.
- **Four services modules have no test file that names them**: services/observability.py (29 lines), services/db_errors.py (87), services/cleanup.py (34), services/embedder.py (55). They sit inside the `--cov=services` measure, so they can drag the gate, but nothing pins their behaviour; whatever coverage they get is incidental to monkeypatched flows.
- **evaluation/summarize_chat_load.py (164 lines) has zero behavioural tests** — only two `is_file()` existence assertions in test_readme_accuracy.py:143 and :214. That is the summarizer the README test *insists* /chat load runs must use, precisely because summarize_jmeter conflates capacity notices with real answers — and summarize_jmeter is the one with real tests (test_jmeter_summary.py). evaluation/chunk_size_sweep.py (314 lines) is untested and outside the coverage measure entirely.
- **MARKERS_WITHOUT_HISTORICAL_ROWS is a hand-curated exemption list.** test_chat_notices.py:48-67 excuses 8+ notice constants from the SQL-backfill audit. :318 only checks the set is a subset of NOTICE_MARKERS; nothing checks that a marker belongs there. The comment at :44-47 admits SYSTEM_ORIGIN_MESSAGE shares its opening clause with the greeting, so the substring check would otherwise have passed by coincidence.
- **The blocking-call audit matches substrings in source segments.** BLOCKING_CALLS (test_event_loop_responsiveness.py:450-461) contains literals like 'sb.table' and 'resolve_effective_department'. Renaming a helper, aliasing an import, or routing a call through a variable silently removes it from the audit. Same class of fragility as the six pinned SELECT field lists, which depend on a regex parse of CREATE TABLE bodies.
- **conftest sets MALWARE_SCAN_MODE=disabled and REQUIRE_PRIVILEGED_MFA=false globally.** Every ClamAV-on and MFA-on path is reached only by per-test monkeypatching of `malware.settings.malware_scan_mode` / `auth.settings.require_privileged_mfa`; there is no end-to-end run with them on. test_auth_authorization has an autouse fixture (:38-48) that forces MFA off and clears both auth caches around every test in the file, so MFA tests must opt back in explicitly.
- **The README's own pytest command is not pinned.** test_readme_accuracy checks links, npm scripts, JMeter plans, the Sonar build and the evidence table, but never compares README.md:289's `--cov=... --cov-fail-under=85` line against quality.yml:70-72. The two can drift.
- **Fixture teardown order bites once, and the fix is commented.** test_guest_budget.py:48-54 captures `guest_budget._storage` before monkeypatching because teardown runs before monkeypatch restores the attribute; clearing through the module would hit the lambda instead of the cached getter.
- **Two tests exist whose only purpose is to make the suite runnable, not to test behaviour**: conftest's interpreter warning, and pytest.ini's Starlette filter — and the second is what makes a wrong-interpreter run look like a library bug. Nothing in CI reproduces that path because CI sets `CI` and the check returns early.
- **`test_the_pinned_lists_cover_the_whole_table_today` (test_hardening_fixes.py:701) asserts equality, so adding any column to profiles, activity_log, chat_sessions, chat_messages, operational_alerts or ingestion_workers fails the suite until the corresponding `_*_FIELDS` string is extended.** That is intentional per its docstring, but it is the most likely test to break on an unrelated migration.
- **No test asserts that `is_stored_non_answer`'s 60-character prefix matcher stays collision-free as new notices are added** — only the one hand-written greeting-vs-provenance case (test_chat_notices.py:260-269).

### Open questions

- Is `models/gemini-embedding-2` in the 20260720 backfill and supabase_setup.sql:354 intentional historical fiction (a name that never existed in production, chosen so legacy rows are deliberately unreadable and must be reindexed) or a typo for `models/gemini-embedding-001`? The behaviour differs materially: the first is a safe fail-closed design, the second silently orphans every pre-provenance chunk. Nothing in the repo says which.
- Should `probe7b.py` be deleted, and should the pylint/coverage target lists be widened to root-level modules so a third scratch file cannot land the same way?
- Are scripts/ and evaluation/ deliberately outside the 85% gate (because they are operational CLIs) or is that an oversight? The evidence file quotes the coverage figure as ISO 25010 evidence, and the harness that produces the Objective 2 result is not in it.
- Will src/data/objective2Summary.json be committed before the frontend build runs in CI? As of this checkout AdminOverview.jsx imports a file git does not track.
- Should a test cross-check Dockerfile:27's `(3, 14, 7)` against README.md:74 and quality.yml:47, the way test_readme_accuracy cross-checks the Sonar build number?
- Should services/citations.py and services/guards.py join the release-fingerprint input list? Both change what a user is served without changing any version constant or any hashed file.

## 4. `docs-paper` - Documentation, paper, evidence and governance

This subsystem is the thesis artifact's evidentiary layer: it is what a defense panel reads, opens and checks, and several parts of it are executable rather than prose. It holds four kinds of thing: (1) narrative documentation whose facts are asserted against the code by PyTest (README.md, the frontend/jmeter READMEs, iso25010_evidence.md); (2) a paper-correction machine (paper/build_corrections.py + _docxlib.py) that regenerates paper_CORRECTED.docx from an untouched 2026-08-09 original with a declared expected-match count per edit, so a wording drift raises instead of silently skipping; (3) a reproducible PowerPoint generator (docs/deck/) that reads every quoted figure out of the evidence files by regex and refuses to build when a rule is violated; and (4) dated, hash-receipted evidence bundles plus the PI-08 governance protocol that gates the twelve-thesis Objective 2 corpus behind four institutional approvals. The repeated design principle is that a claim must trace to a file the panel can open, and that the trace is enforced by code where it can be.

### Key files

- `C:/Users/Kazuha/Desktop/Thesis-V1/README.md` (414 lines / 29.6 KB) - Authoritative setup/operations/evaluation reference. Pinned by tests/test_readme_accuracy.py (32 tests). Documents the 7 migration-only tables, the Objective-2 metric semantics, the `.venv` naming rule, the four-process local stack, the role matrix, the standby-college catalog, the frozen-model defaults, and the 6-job CI table.
- `C:/Users/Kazuha/Desktop/Thesis-V1/CLAUDE.md` (138 lines) - Agent-facing architecture and guardrail file. Carries the frozen-contract list and the 'you changed X -> run Y' test table. Not pinned by any test.
- `C:/Users/Kazuha/Desktop/Thesis-V1/AGENT.md` (3 lines) - 3 lines. Sole content: read and follow ./CLAUDE.md.

### Control flow

The paper pipeline is a pure regenerate-from-original pass. `paper/build_corrections.py:837 build()` opens `paper_ORIGINAL_2026-08-09.docx`, reads only `word/document.xml`, and runs six phases in a fixed order that the edit data depends on. Phase 1 is 13 exact-cell replacements (`_docxlib.replace_cell`, whole-paragraph-text equality so `text-embedding-004` as a Table 3 cell cannot collide with the same token in four prose paragraphs), followed by two `replace_cell_nth(..., 0)` calls that rewrite the embedding name and id. Phase 2 concatenates PROSE through PROSE12 — 49 edits, expect-sum 50 (only the `document loaders` edit expects 2) — and pushes each through `_docxlib.replace_runs:175`, which is the piece that makes the whole thing safe: it tries the literal needle and both apostrophe spellings, maps every `<w:t>` node to its character span in the paragraph's visible text, edits only the nodes the needle overlaps (first node keeps its prefix and receives the whole replacement, interior nodes lose their slice, last keeps its suffix), forces `xml:space="preserve"`, and raises `AssertionError` unless the matched paragraph count equals `expect`. An earlier flatten strategy that pushed the paragraph into run 1 destroyed an italic run-in heading, which is why this exists. Phase 3 appends 32 table rows via `append_table_rows:267`, which resolves exactly one table by anchor text, clones its last *text-bearing* `<w:tr>` as a style template (Table 4 ends in a blank spacer row that would otherwise be cloned), asserts the cell count matches, and inserts directly after the anchor row. Phase 4 moves `Python` above `FastAPI` in Table 2 by first-cell text equality. Phase 5 runs the 10 reference-entry edits through `replace_in_paragraph`, which scopes `replace_runs` to the single paragraph holding an anchor unique to that entry — necessary because fragments like `(4).` and `, et al. (2025).` repeat across the list, and because keeping the edit inside one run is what preserves the italic journal title beside it. Phase 6 deletes the hand-typed spacer runs before Chapter 2/3/References (refusing if anything non-blank is in the span) and then inserts `<w:pageBreakBefore/>` into four headings at the CT_PPr-legal position (after pStyle/keepNext/keepLines, or a fresh `<w:pPr>`). `__main__` then calls `apply_figures:723`, which measures the section's usable text width from pgSz minus pgMar (`text_width_emu`, twips x 635 = 6.27 in here), refits image2/image7 to it preserving native aspect, swaps image1/image8 for the generated PNGs at the same width, and zeroes every floating `wp:positionH/wp:posOffset` — those carried -0.57..-0.76 in offsets tuned to fake-centre over-wide art, so resizing alone left Figures 1 and 8 hanging into the left margin from Pass 4/5 until Pass 9. Finally `set_rel_target` repoints the HiPerRAG hyperlink in `word/_rels/document.xml.rels` (the displayed URL and the live target are stored separately, so text-only would have left the link on the wrong paper) and `replace_parts` rewrites document.xml, the rels part and the two media blobs while copying every other zip entry byte-for-byte with its original ZipInfo. I ran `build()` in memory at HEAD: it completes, so all 50 expected counts still hold.

The deck build is a three-stage gate. `docs/deck/build.py:Ctx.__init__` runs the gates before a single shape exists: `load_comparison()` parses the formal run and `_self_check:154` asserts four invariants (mean of per-row deltas equals the recorded pooled mean to 1e-9; the strata n's sum to the pooled n and to len(rows); `formal_result` is true and queries_scored == queries_total; every `rag_kind` is answer|notice) — so a partial or non-formal run cannot reach a slide at all. `load_iso_block(args.iso_date)` regex-anchors on `^## Local revalidation - <date>, \`<sha>\`` and slices to the next `^## `, then pulls the backend, pylint, frontend, eslint and Playwright figures out of that block only; a missing block is fatal unless `--allow-missing`, in which case slide 9 is stamped `[VERIFY]`. `assert_quoted(C.ISO_QUOTED)` then requires six static strings to occur literally somewhere in the evidence file. `Captures(...)` loads `assets/captures/manifest.json`; `get()` at :97 SystemExits for a missing file unless `--allow-missing` and, independently, SystemExits at :103 for any entry whose `mode` is not `live` unless `--allow-fixture-captures`. `orb.render_all()` (or `logo_renders()` under `--motif logo`) resolves the six motif files and `charts.render_all(cmp)` renders the forest plot and the 40 paired deltas at exactly their placed inch size. Slide functions then flow every box from `measure.block_height_in` and call `check(y, what, limit)` which appends to a module-level `ISSUES` list rather than raising; `pptx_helpers` raises `Overflow` for a forced box that cannot hold its text and `build()` catches it per slide into the same list. If `ISSUES` is non-empty the build prints them and exits 1 — layout failure is a build failure, not a projector surprise. After saving, `xml_post.apply_plan` writes the Morph transition for slides 2-11 as an `mc:AlternateContent` with a `p159:morph` Choice (p14:morph is silently dropped by PowerPoint 365, verified by round-trip) and a mandatory `mc:Fallback` fade, then `inject_spins` builds the `p:timing` tree for the three spin slides. A `.build.json` sidecar records theme, HEAD, the run id, the ISO block date, the used captures, the morph plan and sha256 prefixes of every embedded render. `verify.py` then reopens the file and re-proves 13 rules against the saved bytes, the load-bearing one being that no slide containing `3.222e-05` may omit `0.1257`.

Evidence and governance flow one way. A dated evidence bundle under `docs/evidence/<control>-<timestamp>/` holds machine-readable JSON, a `SHA256SUMS.txt` and a `SUMMARY.md`; a later bundle supersedes an earlier one by saying so in prose (`pi-03-gemini-20260903-045753/SUMMARY.md` explicitly supersedes the 2026-07-25 bundle that recorded `gemini-embedding-2`) and the earlier one is never altered. PI-0x numbering is a release-readiness item, not a document id: PI-03 container/dependency/provider/multiarch security (VERIFIED), PI-04 normalized academic catalog (VERIFIED 2026-07-25 against a disposable Supabase project), PI-05/PI-06 responsive and accessibility release matrices (IMPLEMENTED-UNVERIFIED as of 2026-07-25), PI-08 the corpus lock (BLOCKED-EXTERNAL). PI-08's technical control is `scripts/corpus_manifest.py`: copy the tracked template into the gitignored `evaluation/corpus/private/`, `validate`, then `validate --lock-ready` (which additionally checks the four approval objects, `expected_paper_count == 12`, and that `papers[]` matches `released_composition` exactly — 5 BSCS/Data Mining, 3 BSIS, 4 BLIS, with BSIT/WMAD, BSIT/NETSEC and BSDSA declared unrepresented), then `lock` producing an immutable manifest plus a separate receipt (neither may pre-exist), then `verify` before every formal evaluation. The receipt is the only artefact that enters release evidence.

The Objective-2 figure now has a third consumer beyond the paper and the deck: `scripts/export_objective2_summary.py` derives `rag-thesis-frontend/src/data/objective2Summary.json` from the same pinned run file, the uncommitted `AdminOverview.jsx` change imports that JSON and renders a per-stratum table with a gold "the figure the paper quotes" badge on `present`, and the untracked `tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py` re-runs `build()` and requires byte-equality with the committed file plus explicit assertions that `present` is n=16, delta +0.0601, p=0.1257 and `significant is False`. All three tests suites I ran pass: 32/32 README-accuracy, 3/3 OpenAPI contract, 8/8 objective-2 export, 26/26 corpus manifest.

### Public surface

- paper/build_corrections.py :: build(verbose=True) -> str — applies every edit to word/document.xml in a fixed order (CELLS -> the two nth-cell embedding edits -> PROSE..PROSE12 concatenated -> TABLE_ROWS+TABLE_ROWS9 -> move_table_row -> REFERENCES9 -> DROP_SPACERS -> PAGE_BREAKS) and returns the XML. Verified to run clean at HEAD (582,886 chars).
- paper/build_corrections.py :: apply_figures(xml, src, verbose=True) -> (xml, media) — refits media/image2.png and image7.png to the measured text width (6.27 in), swaps media/image1.png and media/image8.png for the generated PNGs, zeroes every horizontal posOffset. Verified: 4096x1760 -> 6.27x2.69 in and 3360x2860 -> 6.27x5.34 in.
- paper/build_corrections.py :: __main__ — build() -> apply_figures() -> set_rel_target(REL_TARGETS) on word/_rels/document.xml.rels -> replace_parts(SRC, DST, {DOC, RELS, **media}).
- paper/_docxlib.py :: read_xml / write_xml / replace_parts — zip surgery preserving every untouched entry byte-for-byte, with ZipInfo date_time/compress_type/external_attr copied from the source.
- paper/_docxlib.py :: replace_runs(xml, old, new, expect, label) — the primary prose mutator. Tries the literal needle plus both apostrophe spellings, edits only the <w:t> nodes the needle overlaps, forces xml:space="preserve", raises AssertionError unless the paragraph count equals `expect`.
- paper/_docxlib.py :: replace_cell(exact_old, new, expect) / replace_cell_nth(exact_old, new, nth) — whole-paragraph-text equality, so a table cell cannot collide with the same token in prose.
- paper/_docxlib.py :: append_table_rows(xml, anchor, rows, label) — locates the single table containing `anchor`, clones its last text-bearing <w:tr> as a style template, enforces the cell-count match, inserts after that row so a trailing spacer row stays at the bottom.
- paper/_docxlib.py :: move_table_row / replace_in_paragraph / find_paragraph / set_page_break_before / drop_blank_paragraphs_before / set_rel_target / set_drawing_extent / set_drawing_offset / text_width_emu / png_pixels / png_pixels_bytes / media_rid / media_bytes.
- docs/deck/evidence.py :: load_comparison(path=RESULTS_JSON) -> Comparison — typed load + _self_check. Verified output: run 5e8fb7f21db6 at 770bad4, formal=True, 40/40, pooled 0.2169->0.3024 p=3.222e-05, present n=16 +0.0601 p=0.1257 not significant, notice rows [6,7,14,15,20,32,33,36].
- docs/deck/evidence.py :: load_iso_block(date='2026-09-14') -> IsoBlock — regex-extracts backend passed/skipped/coverage/statements/missed, pylint, frontend passed/suites/lines/branches/functions, eslint-clean, Playwright. Verified: 1201/3, 92.07% (5122/406), 10.00/10, 197/7, 96.36/87.09/96.81, clean, 26.
- docs/deck/evidence.py :: assert_quoted(strings, path=EVIDENCE_MD) — raises unless every string occurs literally in the evidence file. Verified PASS for content.ISO_QUOTED.
- docs/deck/evidence.py :: fmt_p / fmt_signed / fmt_ci; dataclasses Stratum, PairedRow, Comparison, IsoBlock; constants STRATUM_LABELS/STRATUM_PLAIN/STRATUM_ORDER.
- docs/deck/build.py :: main(argv) — flags --out, --captures, --allow-fixture-captures, --allow-missing, --iso-date (default 2026-09-14), --motif {orb,logo}.
- docs/deck/build.py :: build(args) -> Path — runs SLIDES 1..11, collects Overflow into ISSUES, exits 1 if any, applies xml_post.apply_plan + inject_spins, saves the PPTX and a sibling .build.json.
- docs/deck/build.py :: Captures.get(key) — manifest lookup; SystemExit for a missing file unless --allow-missing, SystemExit for mode != 'live' unless --allow-fixture-captures.
- docs/deck/build.py :: logo_dir() / logo_renders() — resolve tmp/deck/renders/<mode>-logo and require every name in orb.ALL; never auto-generates.
- docs/deck/build.py :: layout primitives norm/H/T/check, furniture glow/kicker/title/page_number/rail/title_card/grid_card/thumb/frame, and slide_01..slide_11.
- docs/deck/verify.py :: verify(path, draft=False) -> list[(name, ok, detail)] and main() -> int (0/1). 13 checks.
- docs/deck/charts.py :: render_forest / render_paired_deltas / render_all(cmp) — FOREST_SIZE (6.2, 2.8) in and DELTAS_SIZE (4.75, 2.5) in, rendered at exactly the placed size so 14 pt stays 14 pt. p-values deliberately excluded from the PNGs.
- docs/deck/theme.py :: MODE (DECK_THEME env, light default, SystemExit on anything else), two palettes globals()-injected, FONT_TITLE/BODY/MONO, MIN_PT=14, PT role map, RENDER_DIR, pt(name).
- docs/deck/xml_post.py :: MORPH_PLAN (slides 2-11), inject_morph, apply_plan, inject_spins, inject_scene3d, has_morph, spin_targets, TILTED/TILTED_RIGHT/FLAT presets.
- docs/deck/measure.py :: width_in / block_height_in — Pillow ImageFont advance widths, per-face line factors (DM Sans 1.31, Montserrat 1.23, JetBrains Mono 1.32) x 1.04 safety.
- docs/deck/fonts.py :: ensure_fonts() / register_matplotlib() — fetches Montserrat/DM Sans/JetBrains Mono TTFs from the Google Fonts CSS endpoint with a legacy UA into tmp/deck/fonts/.
- docs/deck/orb.py :: FRAMES, ALL (6 names), render_orb / render_glow / render_turntable_gif / render_all(out_dir, force).
- docs/deck/capture.mjs :: CLI --mode live|fixtures, --theme, --keys, --base, --login, --promote/--from, --novelty-file, --zoom, --height, --pause, --retry-fallback, --api. Exported artefacts: assets/captures/*.png, manifest.json, archive_state.json.

- docs/deck/build_defended_slide.py :: build(out) / main(argv) — standalone one-slide closing card using the logo mark as hero; writes tmp/deck/out/IskAI_System_Thesis_Defended.pptx.

- rag-thesis-backend/scripts/export_objective2_summary.py :: build(source=SOURCE) -> dict, constants SOURCE/RUN_ID='5e8fb7f21db6'/RUN_DATE/QUOTED_STRATUM='present'/OUT/STRATUM_LABELS/EVIDENCE_PATH. Writes rag-thesis-frontend/src/data/objective2Summary.json.
- rag-thesis-backend/scripts/corpus_manifest.py :: EXPECTED_PAPER_COUNT = 12; CLI subcommands validate [--lock-ready], lock --manifest-output --receipt-output, verify.

### Invariants

- paper/build_corrections.py always reads `paper_ORIGINAL_2026-08-09.docx` and writes `paper_CORRECTED.docx`. It never reads its own output, so re-running can never double-apply. The original must stay byte-untouched or every needle count is void.
- Every prose/cell/reference edit declares an expected match count and `_docxlib` raises AssertionError on any mismatch — 50 expected paragraph matches across 49 prose edits, 13 cells, 10 reference edits, 1 row move, 4 page breaks, 3 spacer drops. Verified to hold at HEAD.
- Edit ORDER is load-bearing for three lists. PROSE10's needles match the text as PROSE5 leaves it; PROSE11's needle matches the paragraph as PROSE6 leaves it; PROSE7's 'P7-4 same-family judge' anchors on the tail of PROSE2's replacement. PROSE12 is explicitly order-independent because it targets untouched 2026-08-09 text. Reordering the concatenation at build_corrections.py:846 breaks the build loudly, not silently.
- `append_table_rows` requires the anchor to resolve to exactly one table and the new row's cell count to equal the template's; it clones the last *text-bearing* row, never a trailing spacer row.
- `set_page_break_before` inserts `<w:pageBreakBefore/>` only in the CT_PPr-legal position (after pStyle/keepNext/keepLines) or Word repairs the file, and refuses a paragraph that already carries one.
- `drop_blank_paragraphs_before` verifies the span it is about to delete contains nothing but the blank paragraphs it counted, so a correction cannot silently eat content.
- `replace_parts` copies every zip entry it does not replace byte-for-byte, preserving ZipInfo date_time, compress_type and external_attr — the 6 untouched embedded images stay identical.
- docs/deck: no Objective 4 number reaches a slide unless `iso25010_evidence.md` contains it, either inside the requested dated revalidation block (parsed by regex) or literally anywhere in the file (assert_quoted).
- docs/deck/verify.py: the pooled p-value `3.222e-05` may never appear on a slide whose text lacks `0.1257`. This is the deck's central honesty rule and it is enforced per-slide over all shape text, including table cells.
- docs/deck: 11 slides; every non-blank text run carries an explicit size of at least theme.MIN_PT (14); every picture blob resolves; speaker notes on every slide; shape names unique per slide; `!!orb` on slides 1, 10, 11 and as a GIF on 1 and 10; Spin on 2, 3, 11; the Morph plan applied on 2-11 with a fallback and 500-1500 ms durations; no `[CAPTURE]`/`[VERIFY]` markers outside --draft; file under 150 MB.
- docs/deck: Morph is `p159:morph` under `Requires="p159"` with a mandatory `mc:Fallback` fade; the duration attribute is `p14:dur`. `inject_morph` refuses a duration outside 500-1500 ms and an option outside byObject/byWord/byChar.
- docs/deck: persistent objects carry `!!`-prefixed names (`!!orb`, `!!rail1-4`, `!!obj1-4`, `!!frame1-4`, `!!statement`, `!!ring`, `!!glow`, `!!band`, `!!layer1-4`, `!!seal`) so Morph matches them across slides. Renaming one silently loses the transition.
- docs/deck: only live-mode captures may reach a final deck; fixture-mode entries are refused unless --allow-fixture-captures, and verify.py --draft is the matching tolerance.
- evidence.py `_self_check`: per-row deltas must average to the recorded pooled mean difference (1e-9), stratum n's must sum to pooled n and to the row count, the run must be formal and fully scored, and every `rag_kind` must be answer|notice.
- PI-08: exactly 12 papers, four completed approvals, a lock-ready manifest and a receipt that `verify` accepts — `scripts/corpus_manifest.EXPECTED_PAPER_COUNT == 12`, pinned by tests/test_corpus_manifest.py (26 tests, all passing). Researchers may not sign for institutional approvers; PI-08 stays BLOCKED-EXTERNAL until all four registers are filled.
- PI-08: the evaluation corpus is `thesis_category = 'student'` only. A faculty-category manuscript entering the evaluation department during an evaluation window requires a new corpus ID under the §5 change-control procedure.
- PI-08: any later paper change creates a new corpus ID, new approvals and a new receipt; the prior version is never overwritten. `lock` refuses if either output path already exists.
- Controlled material never enters git: `docs/governance/private/` and `rag-thesis-backend/evaluation/corpus/private/` are both gitignored (.gitignore:80-81). Only templates and redacted hash receipts are committed.
- Objective 2 reporting: Section 3.2.5 is pre-specified to quote the `present` stratum. test_objective2_summary_export.py hard-asserts present n=16, delta +0.0601, p=0.1257, significant False, CI straddling zero, and that exactly one stratum is flagged `quoted`.
- The frontend's Objective 2 card must render a generated file, never typed numbers: `src/data/objective2Summary.json` must equal `export_objective2_summary.build()` byte-for-byte, be LF-only and end with a newline.
- docs/evidence bundles are append-only. A superseded bundle is marked superseded in the newer bundle's SUMMARY.md and left unaltered (pi-03-gemini-20260725 vs -20260903).
- README facts are pinned: every migration-only table must be named in the README; every relative link must resolve; all four current JMeter plans must exist and be listed while the superseded one must not be the plan a reader is told to run; every `npm run <script>` the README invokes must exist in package.json; the frontend README's npm version must match `packageManager` and its admin-tab word must match the count of `lazy(() => import('./admin/` in Admin.jsx; the SonarQube build in the README must be 26.7.0.124771 and must not pin the never-used `sonarqube:10.4-community`.

### Data model

- `docs/deck/assets/captures/manifest.json` -> `{captures: {key: {file, route, mode: 'live'|'fixture', theme, captured_at, head, sha256}}}`. 6 entries: landing, chat_grounded, chat_refused, chat_answered (live) and archive, novelty (fixture).
- `docs/deck/assets/captures/archive_state.json` -> `/analytics/summary` verbatim plus `read_on`, `read_at`, `source`. Currently `{total_papers: 16, total_tracks: 4, year_range: {2024, 2026}, total_queries: 197, read_on: '2026-09-14'}`.
- `docs/deck/evidence.Comparison` -> run_id, generated_at, commit, department, formal_result, queries_scored/total, shapiro_w/p, pooled: Stratum, strata: dict[str, Stratum], rows: list[PairedRow], models, prompt_version. `Stratum` = key, n, baseline, rag, mean_diff, ci_lo, ci_hi, p, t, d_z, significant. `PairedRow` = id, coverage, kind, baseline, rag, delta.
- `docs/deck/evidence.IsoBlock` -> date, commit, backend_passed, backend_skipped, coverage_pct, statements, missed, pylint, fe_passed, fe_suites, fe_lines, fe_branches, fe_functions, eslint_clean, playwright_passed, raw.
- `<deck>.build.json` sidecar -> built_at, theme, head, objective2_run, iso_block, captures (the used subset of the manifest), morph_plan, spins (always `{}`), renders (name -> sha256[:16] for the 6 motif files + 2 charts), size_bytes.
- `rag-thesis-frontend/src/data/objective2Summary.json` -> `_generated_by`, `_do_not_edit`, `run{id, date, generated_at, git_commit, prompt_version, formal_result, queries_scored, queries_total, department, framework, judge_model, answer_model, metric, evidence_path}`, `quoted_stratum`, `strata[5]{key, label, baseline, rag, n, delta, p_value, significant, cohens_d_z, ci_95[2], quoted}`, `diagnostics{faithfulness, context_precision}{mean, n}`.
- `rag-thesis-backend/evaluation/corpus/corpus_manifest.template.json` -> approvals (4 gates), corpus_id, department, expected_paper_count (12), papers[], processing_profile, purpose, released_composition{note, released_on '2026-09-07', represented[{count, program, specialization}], unrepresented[]}, schema_version, status ('draft').
- `docs/evidence/<control>/SUMMARY.md` + `SHA256SUMS.txt` + machine-readable JSON — the evidence-bundle triple. Bundles: pi-03-20260725-044127 (Trivy 0.72.0, 0/0 High/Critical on 3 images), pi-03-dependencies-20260725-045627, pi-03-gemini-{20260725-043908, 20260903-045753, 20260903-051421}, pi-03-multiarch-20260725-044816 (amd64/arm64 OCI digests), pi-04/{manual-20260725 (6 PNGs), live-20260725-051646}, security/audit-2026-09-05 (8 files).
- `docs/evidence/contracts/` -> `iskai-openapi-2026-07-25.json` (immutable dated snapshot, sha256 0074471…) and `iskai-openapi.current.json` (regenerated per endpoint change, diffed by tests/test_export_openapi.py).
- `paper/` artefact set -> paper_ORIGINAL_2026-08-09.docx (6,007,769 B, never edited), paper_CORRECTED.docx (6,342,244 B, rebuilt 2026-09-14), paper_CORRECTED.pdf (1,501,434 B, 64 pages, exported 2026-09-13), figures/{figure1_rag_architecture.png 4096x1760, figure8_system_architecture.png 3360x2860, figure_ai_pipeline.png (orphan)}.

### Config knobs

- `DECK_THEME` = light (default) | dark — read once at `docs/deck/theme.py:18` import time, `SystemExit` on any other value. Selects the palette, the chart colours and `RENDER_DIR` (tmp/deck/renders/<mode>).
- `docs/deck/build.py --iso-date` (default `2026-09-14`) — which dated revalidation block the Objective 4 figures come from.
- `docs/deck/build.py --motif` = orb (default) | logo — swaps the whole motif render set; rebinds the module-level `RENDERS` in `main()` only.
- `docs/deck/build.py --allow-missing` / `--allow-fixture-captures` / `--out` / `--captures`; `verify.py --draft`.
- `capture.mjs` --mode live|fixtures, --theme, --base (5173 live / 4173 fixtures), --keys, --login, --promote/--from, --novelty-file, --zoom, --height, --pause (25000 ms, free-tier per-minute quota), --retry-fallback (3), --api (8000), --state.
- `export_slides.ps1` -Deck / -OutDir / -Width (1920).
- Objective 2 formal-run settings the README makes mandatory: `LLM_BASE_URL` unset, `APP_ENVIRONMENT=development`, `GUEST_DAILY_TOKEN_BUDGET=0`, never `--allow-unvalidated`.
- `BACKUP_RPO_HOURS` (default 0 = staleness check disabled; set to 24 only after the first backup is recorded) and `BACKUP_RTO_HOURS` (recorded, never enforced).
- `RETENTION_ENFORCEMENT_ENABLED` — the apply request is rejected unless true; deliberately off pending institutional approval.
- `MALWARE_SCAN_MODE=disabled` — local development only; production config validation rejects it.
- `ALLOW_DISPOSABLE_SUPABASE_TESTS=1` — opts the RLS/queue integration tests in; its literal name is asserted to appear in iso25010_evidence.md so the table states which counting convention it used.
- `SONAR_TOKEN` / `SONAR_HOST_URL` — absent, the SonarQube CI job skips gracefully and the rest of the gate still runs.
- `.gitignore`: `tmp/`, `docs/governance/private/`, `rag-thesis-backend/evaluation/corpus/private/`; `docs/deck/.gitignore` is `__pycache__/` only.
- `docs/deck/requirements-deck.txt` — python-pptx 1.0.2, lxml 6.1.3, matplotlib 3.10.8, numpy 2.5.3, pillow 12.1.0, installed into a venv deliberately separate from `rag-thesis-backend/.venv`.

### Error handling

- `_docxlib` raises `AssertionError` with the edit's label and the actual count on any expected-match mismatch; `set_drawing_extent`/`set_drawing_offset` raise `ValueError` unless exactly one `wp:extent`+`a:ext` / one `posOffset` is found; `media_rid` raises `LookupError`; `replace_parts` raises `KeyError` for a zip part that does not exist. The build is all-or-nothing: nothing is written until every edit has succeeded.
- `docs/deck/build.py` accumulates layout problems in a module-level `ISSUES` list (from `check()` and from `Overflow` caught per slide) and only then prints them and `raise SystemExit(1)` — so one run reports every overflow rather than the first.
- `pptx_helpers.Overflow` is raised for a forced-height text box that cannot hold its measured text; `build_chapter1_packet.draw_cover` raises `SystemExit` with the needed point count when `insert_textbox` returns a negative.
- Missing or fixture captures, a missing ISO revalidation block, and missing logo motif files all `raise SystemExit` with the exact remedial command. `--allow-missing` downgrades the capture and ISO-block failures to `[VERIFY]`/`[CAPTURE]` placeholders; `--allow-fixture-captures` downgrades the mode check; `verify.py --draft` tolerates the resulting markers.
- `evidence._self_check` raises `ValueError` for a non-formal, partially scored, or internally inconsistent run. `load_iso_block` raises `ValueError` naming the missing row. `assert_quoted` raises `ValueError` listing every string absent from the evidence file.
- `verify.py` returns a (name, ok, detail) triple per rule, prints PASS/FAIL aligned, and exits 1 if any failed; it reconfigures stdout to UTF-8 first because Windows consoles default to cp1252.
- `corpus_manifest validate --lock-ready` returns a list of human-readable errors rather than a single failure (the 2026-07-25 audit records the exact five-line output); `lock` refuses if either output already exists or if the two outputs are the same path; `verify` detects manifest tampering.
- `capture.mjs` catches per-key failures, logs `FAILED <key>: <message>` and continues, so one broken surface does not lose the rest of a run; `recordArchiveState` swallows a fetch failure and logs `archive state not recorded`. The admin captures throw explicitly on a 2FA gate.
- `logo_renders()` refuses rather than falling back to the orb, on the stated grounds that a half-orb half-logo deck is worse than a refusal.
- `build_chapter1_packet.main` returns 1 with `missing <SOURCE>; run paper/build_corrections.py first` and raises `SystemExit` if a requested page is past the PDF's page count.

### Tests that pin it

- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_readme_accuracy.py — 32 tests, all passing. Pins: migration-only tables named in the README; the base schema is genuinely insufficient; `upload_jobs` is in the base schema; the baseline is scored only on answer_correctness (`baseline_entry = {'answer_correctness'` and exactly 2 construction sites) and no `baseline_faithfulness`/`baseline_context_precision` exists; every relative README link resolves; all four current JMeter plans exist and are listed and the superseded one is not the plan a reader is told to run; both summarizers exist; every `npm run <script>` the README invokes exists; the frontend README's npm version matches `packageManager` and its admin-tab word matches `lazy(() => import('./admin/` count in Admin.jsx; test:coverage and bundle:budget are documented; the JMeter README invokes the jar not the .bat and points /chat runs at summarize_chat_load; four superseded ISO figures are absent from the current table; `ALLOW_DISPOSABLE_SUPABASE_TESTS` is mentioned in the evidence file; SonarQube 26.7.0.124771 is in both the evidence and the README and `sonarqube:10.4-community` is in neither; both Sonar coverage report paths are configured.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py (UNTRACKED) — 8 tests, all passing. Byte-equality of the committed JSON with `build()`; LF-only with trailing newline; run id/date/formal_result/40-of-40/ragas==0.4.3; pooled 0.2169->0.3024 p=3.222e-05 significant n=40; quoted stratum is `present`, n=16, delta +0.0601, p=0.1257, NOT significant, CI straddles zero; exactly one stratum flagged quoted; 5 strata each with a bool flag, n>=1 and a human label; the n=4 and n=3 strata are still exported.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_corpus_manifest.py — 26 tests, all passing. `EXPECTED_PAPER_COUNT == 12`; `released_composition` is validated not decorative (parametrised mutations); lock-readiness rejects records contradicting the composition, incomplete/inconsistent records and an invalid approval date; the template is a valid unlocked draft; lock sorts records, writes the receipt and verifies; lock refuses to overwrite immutable artifacts and requires distinct outputs and a tz-aware timestamp; verify detects manifest tampering.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_export_openapi.py — 3 tests, all passing. Diffs the live app's OpenAPI against `docs/evidence/contracts/iskai-openapi.current.json`; the error message names the regeneration command and notes the dated snapshots are immutable.
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_schema_consistency.py — pins the known base-vs-migration function-body differences (referenced by CLAUDE.md's change table).
- rag-thesis-backend/tests/test_rag_controls.py::TestRequestGuard — verified to collect exactly **40** parametrised cases, which is the number the walkthrough, RUN_OF_SHOW and content.py all cite as the refusal guard's evidence.
- docs/evidence/security/audit-2026-09-05/test_audit_reproductions.py — 14 offline cases reproducing F02-F12 as *defects*. Deliberately NOT in the test suite; its README says it must stay separate until its expectations are inverted to assert corrected behaviour. Results retained as reproductions.xml.
- docs/deck/verify.py — not a PyTest file, but the deck's own 13-rule acceptance gate, run as `python -m docs.deck.verify` and exiting 1 on any failure.
- No test pins CLAUDE.md, DEFENSE_WALKTHROUGH.md, RUN_OF_SHOW.md, the runbooks, the PI-08 protocol, CORRECTIONS_APPLIED.md, or anything under docs/deck/. The paper build is self-checking (expected match counts) but has no PyTest wrapper either.

### Gotchas

- **The committed deck cannot build a final deck today.** `docs/deck/assets/captures/manifest.json` records `archive` and `novelty` as `"mode": "fixture"`. `build.py:103` SystemExits on any non-live capture without `--allow-fixture-captures`, and both keys are consumed by slide 3 (`build.py:339`) and slide 7 (`build.py:483-484`). So `python -m docs.deck.build` with no flags aborts on `archive`. `--allow-missing` does not rescue it — the mode check runs after the existence check. docs/deck/README.md's capture section tells you to `--promote archive,novelty --from live-light`; that has not been done.
- **`assert_quoted` cannot tell a fresh figure from a superseded one.** It searches the whole of `iso25010_evidence.md`, archived blocks included. Three of the six `ISO_QUOTED` strings (`p95 204 ms`, `55 scans`, `60 requests in 14 seconds`) are satisfied only from the **2026-08-04** block at iso25010_evidence.md:1070/1118/1124, and `Duplication 1.3%` from the 2026-09-01 block at :845. The guard passes; the figures are old.
- **The `55 scans` accessibility figure is now wrong.** `e2e/accessibility.spec.js:323` defines **12** surfaces (landing, authentication, guest-chat, dashboard, archive, novelty, upload, upload-batch, admin-overview, admin-upload-history, admin-system-management, admin-operations) and the loop at :484-511 runs 4 THEME_STATES at 1280 px plus dark-standard at 360 px = 5 scans/surface = **60**. DEFENSE_WALKTHROUGH.md §6 and `content.py:114` both say `55 scans — 11 surfaces × 4 …`. README.md correctly says "the 12-surface axe accessibility matrix", so the README and the walkthrough now disagree with each other.
- **`5ed9539` — the commit the deck's own Objective 4 provenance line names — does not exist in this repository.** `git cat-file -t 5ed9539` -> "Not a valid object name". It is the sha in `iso25010_evidence.md:9` (`## Local revalidation - 2026-09-14, \`5ed9539\``), which `evidence.load_iso_block` reads and `build.py` prints on slide 9 as "test counts re-run 2026-09-14 at 5ed9539". Every other commit cited across the docs (da4e60f, 770bad4, 9cb3659, 4847209, cc886e5, 1426252…, da9e931, 3e003aa, a802d35, 13f10c6, 347aa86, 733e186, 2b29f9a, 9dc343c, a50b59b, 7852349, 4d216ea, a1893b1, e717acb, cb4842b) resolves.
- **The whole Objective 4 quality table is ~940 tests behind HEAD.** The 2026-09-14 block and DEFENSE_WALKTHROUGH §6 record "1,201 passed"; `pytest --collect-only` at HEAD collects **2,143**. Four commits on 2026-09-14 (b7199bb, 576f59f, e751bbc, f5a90cf) added `test_chat_routing_multilingual.py`, `test_guards_multilingual.py`, `test_followup_multilingual.py`, `test_query_translation.py` and more. The deck reads 1,201 out of the file and prints it on slide 9.
- **CLAUDE.md's `~2,135 tests` is also stale, by exactly 8** — the count of the untracked `tests/test_objective2_summary_export.py`. 2143 - 2135 = 8.
- **CLAUDE.md overstates the release fingerprint's inputs.** It says `scripts/release_fingerprint.py` hashes `services/prompts.py`, `services/chunker.py` and `services/index_provenance.py`. Only `prompts.py` is in the `inputs` list (release_fingerprint.py:51-71, which is requirements.txt, requirements.lock, config.py, routers/chat.py, services/prompts.py, services/retriever.py, services/question_types.py, frontend package-lock.json, both Dockerfiles, docker-compose.operations.yml). `CHUNKING_VERSION` and `PREPROCESSING_VERSION` reach the manifest only as *values* inside `index_fingerprint`, so an edit to chunker.py or index_provenance.py that does not move those constants leaves the manifest byte-identical.
- **`services/citations.py` is genuinely unhashed by the fingerprint** — confirmed against the input list. RUN_OF_SHOW.md:210-211 volunteers this and the 2026-09-12 audit (A05) records it; `evaluation/run_comparison.py` itself is also outside the compared set, which the audit calls "F10 one level up".
- **`paper_CORRECTED.pdf` is genuinely stale, but not for the reason the log gives.** CORRECTIONS_APPLIED.md says twice that Passes 10 and 12 did not regenerate it — yet the committed PDF *does* contain the Pass 10, 11 and 12 wording (verified by extracting text with PyMuPDF). What it still contains is `empirically optimized`, the phrase an **undocumented 2026-09-14 pass** (commit 576f59f) removed from PROSE2's 'P2-7 tokenizer caveat and chunk size basis' edit in favour of the chunk-size-sweep wording. DOCX mtime 2026-09-14 17:03, PDF mtime 2026-09-13 20:04.
- **CORRECTIONS_APPLIED.md is missing two passes.** There is no `## Pass 11` section although `PROSE11` (the 99.9% verbatim re-submission refusal, dated 2026-09-09 in the source comment) exists and is applied, and no Pass 13 section for the 2026-09-14 chunk-size-sweep rewrite that cites `evaluation/chunk_size_sweep.py` and `evaluation/results/chunk_size_sweep_2026-09-14.json` (both present on disk). The log also still records "Rows added (26)" and "61 pages"; the build now appends 32 rows and the PDF is 64 pages.
- **`docs/evidence/PI_VERIFICATION_2026-07-25.md` contradicts the current corpus rule and is not marked superseded.** It states PI-08 requires "exactly 50 real CCSICT theses" (lines 18, 102, 108) and records a live Gemini smoke on `gemini-embedding-2` (line 39). The protocol, the template and `corpus_manifest.EXPECTED_PAPER_COUNT` all say 12, and the embedding model has been `gemini-embedding-001` since 2026-09-03. Both are historically correct for that date, but the file carries no supersession banner the way the pi-03-gemini bundles do.
- **The new `--motif logo` switch has a bypass.** `build.py:722` rebinds the module-level `RENDERS` global inside `main()` only, while `Ctx.__init__:151` picks the render *set* from `args.motif`. A programmatic `build(args)` call with `motif='logo'` therefore embeds orb art while hashing logo files into the `.build.json` sidecar — exactly the "sidecar quietly wrong" failure the new comment at :148-150 says it is preventing.
- **Dead code in the deck provenance sidecar.** `build.py:698` is `'spins': {str(SLIDES.index(fn) + 1): None for fn in ()}` — it iterates the empty tuple, so the key is always `{}`. The spin plan is never recorded despite `ctx.spins` being populated.
- **`logo_motif.py` destroys its own inputs.** `assemble()` deletes `_frames/` and `_frames.json` after writing the GIF, so a second `python -m docs.deck.logo_motif` run fails with "no frames in …"; you must re-run `node docs/deck/logo_motif.mjs` first.
- **The capture manifest's `captured_at` and `head` are promotion-time, not capture-time.** `capture.mjs:promote` stamps `new Date().toISOString()` and `git rev-parse --short HEAD` at promote time — which is why `chat_grounded/refused/answered` all read `02:12:03.401/.402/.403`. docs/deck/README.md describes the manifest as recording the capture's "timestamp, commit". Also, `theme` is derived as `from.split('-')[1] || 'dark'`, so `--from live` (no theme suffix) silently records `dark`.
- **Four Figure build scripts, all emitting SVG; the paper consumes PNG.** `Figure1_RAG_Architecture.build.py` and `Figure8_System_Architecture.build.py` write `.svg` to the CWD, but `build_corrections.FIGURES` loads `figures/figure1_rag_architecture.png` and `figures/figure8_system_architecture.png`. The SVG->PNG rasterisation step is nowhere in the repo, and `Figure8_System_Architecture.svg` is not even committed (only Figure1's is). Figure8's docstring still says `python build_figure8.py`, a filename that no longer exists.
- **Three orphan figure artefacts.** The untracked `Figure_System_Architecture.build.py` (phase-card architecture) and `Figure_System_Overview.build.py` (plain-language two-flows overview) emit `IskAI_System_Architecture.svg` / `IskAI_System_Overview.svg` and are referenced by nothing else in the repo. The untracked `paper/figures/figure_ai_pipeline.png` is referenced by nothing at all — a repo-wide grep finds only the two build scripts' own docstrings.
- **Dead API in `_docxlib.py`.** `write_xml`, `replace_text`, `replace_smart` and `paragraphs_nested` are never called from `build_corrections.py`. `replace_text` survives only as `replace_smart`'s implementation and `replace_smart` has no caller; `paragraphs_nested` was written for the five paragraphs nested inside `w:txbxContent`/`mc:AlternateContent` (including all of §3.2.4) and is unused, which means the shallow `paragraphs()` regex — which ends an outer paragraph at the first `</w:p>` — is what every live edit path actually uses.
- **`build_chapter1_packet.py` hardcodes page ranges into a document that moves.** SECTIONS pins pp. 1, 3-4, 16, 48-51, 56-59 of `paper_CORRECTED.pdf`. The only validation is `last > src.page_count`; nothing checks the extracted page contains the named section. I verified the ranges still land correctly on the current 64-page PDF (3.3 heading is on p56, Figure 8 caption on p59, Figure 1 on p16, 3.2.3 on p48), but Passes 8/9/10/12 already moved the document from 62 -> 61 -> 64 pages and any re-export can silently shift them. The cover records the source SHA-256, so a panelist could at least detect the mismatch after the fact.
- **A stale sentence inside the brand-new AdminOverview comment.** The uncommitted comment block says "Still deliberately no figures" immediately above a table that renders baseline, RAG, change, p and n for five strata. The prose is a leftover from the version of the change that kept the card empty.
- **`assert_quoted`'s counterpart for line references has no guard.** `content.py:67` cites `DEFENSE_WALKTHROUGH.md 113-127`, `content.py:128` cites `276-343` for the limitations (now at line 298) and `capture.mjs:40` cites `447-462` for the own-thesis note (now at 496). The uncommitted +33-line edit at §3.7 shifted everything below it.
- **The backup drill has never been run.** `docs/BACKUP_RESTORE_DRILL.md` measured-results table has one row reading `_(pending)_`, all six operator checklist items are unchecked, and `BACKUP_RPO_HOURS` defaults to `0` which disables the staleness alert entirely. The RTO target is explicitly recorded-not-enforced.
- **`archive_state.json` is the demo's most volatile number and it is baked into the deck.** It reads 16 papers / 4 tracks / 2024-2026 as of 2026-09-14 and slide 7 quotes it with the read date. Both RUN_OF_SHOW and the walkthrough tell the presenters to read `/analytics/summary` aloud on the day instead, and to be ready for "why 16 manuscripts when the paper says 12?" — the evaluated corpus and the deployed archive are deliberately different sets.
- **`fonts.py` needs the network at build time.** It fetches Montserrat/DM Sans/JetBrains Mono from the Google Fonts CSS endpoint with a spoofed legacy UA; `measure.py` and `charts.py` both depend on the TTFs, so an offline machine cannot build or even measure the deck.
- **`tmp/` does not exist in this working tree**, so there are no deck renders, no built PPTX, no `notes.json`, and no `tmp/defense/Chapter1_Defense_Packet.pdf` — every artefact RUN_OF_SHOW.md tells the presenter to open must be regenerated first.
- **CI job count reconciliation.** `.github/workflows/quality.yml` defines 6 jobs (backend, frontend-audit, frontend, secrets, containers, sonarqube) and README's table lists 6 rows, but DEFENSE_WALKTHROUGH §6 says "7 jobs, all green". Both are right: `containers` carries a 2-entry matrix (backend, frontend), so the check-runs API reports 7. The walkthrough says it measured "the CI row against the GitHub check-runs API".
- **The 2026-09-05 audit's evidence directory is deliberately not a regression suite.** `docs/evidence/security/audit-2026-09-05/test_audit_reproductions.py` asserts the *defective* behaviour of F02-F12 and its README says it must stay out of `tests/` until its expectations are inverted. Eight of its twelve findings were still open when the 2026-09-12 review ran; F03, F04 and F07 are recorded as closed-by-reading-only, and F06 (guest-ID rotation resetting per-guest limits) is accepted by design.

### Open questions

- What is `5ed9539`? The commit named by the 2026-09-14 ISO revalidation block — the block the deck reads and prints — is not reachable in this repository or on either remote. Either the block needs re-stamping against a commit that exists, or the measurement needs re-running.
- Should the Objective 4 table be re-measured before the final defense? It records 1,201 backend tests at a commit that does not exist; HEAD collects 2,143 after four 2026-09-14 feature commits. The deck quotes the stale number, and the walkthrough presents it as 'measured by exit code' evidence.
- Why are `archive` and `novelty` still fixture captures? Every slide-3 and slide-7 screenshot in a final deck must be live, and the build refuses as designed — but nobody has promoted live ones, so no non-draft deck can currently be produced.
- The `55 scans` / `11 surfaces` accessibility figure needs re-deriving against the current 12-surface spec (60 scans), and the README already disagrees with the walkthrough on the surface count.
- The walkthrough calls the fourth-decimal disagreement between the export and the evidence table 'unexplained', but iso25010_evidence.md:34-40 does explain it — the markdown table transcribes 0.1990/0.3600 and 0.2622/0.3761 where the JSON holds 0.1991/0.3601 and 0.2616/0.3755, and declares the JSON authoritative. Either the walkthrough sentence should be narrowed to 'why the table was transcribed differently' or dropped.
- Should CORRECTIONS_APPLIED.md gain the missing Pass 11 and Pass 13 sections, and should the PDF be re-exported so `empirically optimized` stops appearing in the circulated artefact?
- Should `services/citations.py` and `evaluation/run_comparison.py` be added to `release_fingerprint.build_manifest()`'s input set? Both the 2026-09-12 audit and RUN_OF_SHOW volunteer the gap; the audit deliberately defers it to a manifest schema-version bump.
- Should CLAUDE.md's claim that the fingerprint hashes chunker.py and index_provenance.py be corrected, or should those files actually be added to the input list?
- Should `docs/evidence/PI_VERIFICATION_2026-07-25.md` carry a supersession banner (50 papers -> 12, `gemini-embedding-2` -> `gemini-embedding-001`), matching the pattern the pi-03-gemini bundles already use?
- Is the SVG->PNG rasterisation of Figures 1 and 8 reproducible? The step is undocumented, Figure 8's SVG is not committed, and Figure 8's docstring names a script filename that no longer exists.
- Is `paper/figures/figure_ai_pipeline.png` meant to become a paper figure, or is it scratch that should be gitignored along with the two untracked Figure_System_*.build.py scripts?
- When is the first backup restore drill? The declared RPO/RTO have no measured row, `BACKUP_RPO_HOURS` defaults to 0 (alerting off), and the walkthrough's own limitation list does not mention it.
- Do the `build_chapter1_packet.py` page ranges need a content assertion (e.g. the extracted page must contain the section heading) rather than only a page-count bound, given the manuscript has already moved 62 -> 61 -> 64 pages?
